Section ID: B1 Section Name: Knowledge & Intelligence Organizational Model Platform ID: PD-04 Official Name: Knowledge & Intelligence Platform Authority: Knowledge Authority Volume: Volume 4 Part: Part B — Organization Architecture Predecessor: Part A — Platform Identity & Strategic Foundation Status: FROZEN — Bounded Canonical Synthesis Classification: Organization Architecture Source-Fidelity Mode: Bounded Reconstruction with Source Gap Gold Standard Review: PASS WITH SOURCE QUALIFICATION Freeze Decision: APPROVED WITH SOURCE QUALIFICATION

⸻

1\. Purpose

B1 menetapkan model organisasi tingkat tinggi PD-04 Knowledge & Intelligence sebagai dasar untuk menerjemahkan mandat Knowledge & Intelligence menjadi organizational ownership yang jelas.

Jika Part A menjawab:

Mengapa PD-04 ada, apa mandatnya, apa authority-nya, di mana boundary-nya, dan apa success condition-nya?

maka B1 menjawab:

Bagaimana PD-04 harus dibentuk sebagai organisasi agar mandat Knowledge & Intelligence dapat dijalankan secara coherent, bounded, dan accountable?

B1 menjadi parent architectural layer bagi:

• Sub Division Architecture;

• Capability Architecture;

• Team Architecture;

• Role Group Architecture;

• Reporting Hierarchy;

• Organizational Interfaces;

• Accountability & Responsibility;

• Organization Evolution & Maturity;

• Organization Success.

B1 tidak menetapkan daftar Sub Division, Team, atau Role Group secara premature.

⸻

• Knowledge;

4\. Organizational Model Constitution

Model organisasi PD-04 harus mengikuti:

Mandate

↓

Responsibility

↓

Organizational Ownership

↓

Capability

↓

Execution Structure

Bukan:

Existing Structure

↓

Dengan demikian, struktur organisasi PD-04 harus muncul dari Knowledge & Intelligence responsibility analysis, bukan dari penyalinan struktur Platform lain.

5\. Organizational Design Principles

OD-01 — Domain Before Structure

PD-04 terlebih dahulu mempertahankan domain:

Knowledge & Intelligence

baru kemudian melakukan decomposition organizational.

⸻

OD-02 — Responsibility Before Organizational Unit

Setiap organizational unit harus memiliki:

• purpose;

• responsibility;

• ownership;

• boundary;

• outputs;

• consumers;

• interfaces;

• accountability.

Tidak ada unit yang menjadi canonical hanya karena namanya terdengar relevan.

⸻

OD-03 — Ownership Before Assignment

Model dasar:

Knowledge Domain

↓

Owner

↓

Responsibility

↓

Capability

↓

Team

↓

Role Group

Pattern Organization Architecture tersebut konsisten dengan reference organization model yang digunakan dalam Platform Organization.

Cabang Cabang Cabang Cabang Cabang Cabang Cabang Cabang Cabang .txt

⸻

OD-04 — One Primary Owner

Setiap material organizational responsibility harus memiliki satu primary owner.

Collaboration dapat melibatkan banyak unit.

Namun:

Collaboration does not create multiple primary owners.

⸻

OD-05 — Knowledge Domain Integrity

PD-04 harus mempertahankan primary domain:

Knowledge & Intelligence

Struktur organisasi tidak boleh berkembang menjadi general AIOS strategy, runtime, infrastructure, governance, atau engineering organization.

⸻

OD-06 — Authority Does Not Equal Structure

Knowledge Authority tidak otomatis menentukan:

• jumlah Sub Division;

• jumlah Team;

• jumlah Role Group;

• reporting line;

• workforce composition.

Dengan demikian:

Knowledge Authority

≠

Organizational Unit

Authority tetap mengikuti constitutional dan governance boundary.

⸻

OD-07 — Dependency Does Not Transfer Ownership

PD-04 dapat memiliki dependency terhadap:

• Architecture;

• Governance;

• Runtime;

• AI Engineering;

• Platform lain.

Tetapi:

Dependency

≠

Ownership

Document Relationship Matrix secara eksplisit menempatkan Architecture dan Governance sebagai relationship PD-04 tanpa menghapus domain ownership Platform tersebut.

Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

⸻

OD-08 — Minimum Necessary Structure

PD-04 tidak boleh menciptakan organizational layers tanpa material responsibility.

Struktur harus cukup untuk:

• menjalankan Knowledge mandate;

• menjaga ownership;

• menjaga accountability;

• mendukung capability;

• menjaga domain boundary;

• mendukung evolution.

⸻

OD-09 — Stable Domain, Evolvable Decomposition

Domain harus relatif stabil.

Decomposition dapat berkembang:

Stable Domain

↓

Stable Ownership

↓

Evolving Capability

↓

Evolving Team

↓

Evolving Role Group

Perubahan Team tidak otomatis berarti perubahan Platform boundary.

⸻

OD-10 — Organization Before Workforce

B1 mendefinisikan organization architecture.

B1 tidak menetapkan:

• individu;

• headcount;

• employment type;

• recruitment;

• compensation;

• personal appointment.

Workforce architecture mengikuti organizational architecture.

⸻

17\. Knowledge Ownership Boundary

PD-04 dapat memiliki ownership terhadap Knowledge domain dan canonical Knowledge assets yang memang ditetapkan sebagai bagian dari domain tersebut.

Namun:

Tidak setiap data yang digunakan oleh AIOS otomatis menjadi PD-04 property.

Ownership harus ditentukan berdasarkan:

• domain;

• responsibility;

• source;

• authority;

• lifecycle;

• architectural decision.

⸻

18\. Context Boundary

Context merupakan bagian dari domain PD-04 sejauh context tersebut merupakan bagian dari Knowledge & Intelligence responsibility.

Namun:

Context

≠

Runtime State

Runtime state atau execution context yang menjadi bagian dari Runtime domain tidak otomatis menjadi Knowledge organizational asset.

Boundary harus ditetapkan secara eksplisit pada architecture/interface layer yang relevan.

⸻

19\. Intelligence Asset Boundary

PD-04 dapat memiliki organizational responsibility atas Intelligence Assets.

Namun:

Intelligence Asset

≠

AI Agent

PD-04 tidak mengambil ownership terhadap Agent Engineering hanya karena intelligence asset dikonsumsi oleh Agent.

33\. Canonical Freeze Statement

B1 — Knowledge & Intelligence Organizational Model establishes the organizational architecture foundation through which PD-04 translates its Knowledge & Intelligence mandate into accountable organizational ownership while preserving Knowledge Authority, Platform autonomy, cross-platform boundaries, and the separation between organization, governance, operation, and workforce implementation.

B1 establishes the organizational model but does not predetermine the number or names of Sub Divisions, Capabilities, Teams, or Role Groups. Those structures must be derived through subsequent responsibility, capability, boundary, and ownership analysis.

The available source material establishes PD-04 identity, Knowledge Authority, and Knowledge & Intelligence domain positioning, but does not establish a complete literal B1 organizational model. Therefore this section is frozen as a Bounded Canonical Synthesis with an explicit source-investigation requirement for Claude Code.

B1 is FROZEN.

4\. B2 Design Constitution

B2 menggunakan prinsip:

SD-01 — Responsibility Before Structure

Sub Division tidak boleh dibuat hanya berdasarkan nama yang terlihat masuk akal.

Setiap Sub Division harus muncul dari responsibility yang material.

Responsibility

↓

Sub Division

SD-02 — Ownership Before Assignment

Setiap Sub Division harus memiliki:

• primary responsibility;

• ownership;

• boundary;

• outputs;

• consumers;

• interfaces.

⸻

SD-03 — One Primary Owner

Setiap material responsibility memiliki satu primary organizational owner.

Namun:

Collaboration does not create multiple primary owners.

⸻

SD-04 — Domain Integrity

Seluruh Sub Division PD-04 harus tetap berada di bawah:

Knowledge & Intelligence

Sub Division tidak boleh berkembang menjadi:

• Runtime organization;

• AI Engineering organization;

• Infrastructure organization;

• Governance organization;

• Security organization;

• Quality organization;

• Developer Experience organization.

⸻

SD-05 — Minimum Necessary Structure

Jangan membuat Sub Division jika responsibility tersebut dapat dikelola secara coherent tanpa organizational separation.

Tujuan B2 bukan memaksimalkan jumlah unit.

Tujuannya adalah:

clear ownership with minimum unnecessary organizational complexity.

⸻

SD-06 — Separation of Concern

Responsibility dapat dipisahkan menjadi Sub Division berbeda apabila terdapat material difference pada:

• objective;

• ownership;

• lifecycle;

36\. Canonical Freeze Statement

B2 — Knowledge & Intelligence Sub Division Architecture establishes the organizational container layer through which PD-04 translates its Knowledge, Context, and Intelligence Assets responsibilities into explicit organizational ownership while preserving Knowledge Authority, Platform autonomy, and cross-platform domain boundaries.

The available source material establishes the PD-04 Knowledge & Intelligence domain and its responsibility relationship to Knowledge, Context, and Intelligence Assets, but does not establish a literal final PD-04 Sub Division structure. Therefore, any concrete Sub Division naming or decomposition in this section is classified as bounded reconstruction rather than historical source transcription.

No Sub Division may become canonical solely because it is architecturally plausible. A final Sub Division requires responsibility, ownership, boundary, output, consumer, interface, and traceability evidence, or an explicit authoritative Architecture Decision.

B2 is FROZEN with explicit source qualification.

SD-01 — Responsibility Before Structure

• interfaces.

SD-03 — One Primary Owner

Setiap material responsibility memiliki satu primary organizational owner.

Kolaborasi antar Sub Division diperbolehkan.

Namun:

Collaboration does not create multiple primary owners.

Pattern one-primary-owner ini konsisten dengan organization architecture source.

Knowledge & Intelligence

• Infrastructure organization;

• Governance organization;

• Security organization;

• Quality organization;

• Developer Experience organization.

⸻

SD-05 — Minimum Necessary Structure

Jangan membuat Sub Division jika responsibility tersebut dapat dikelola secara coherent tanpa organizational separation.

Tujuan B2 bukan memaksimalkan jumlah unit.

Tujuannya adalah:

clear ownership with minimum unnecessary organizational complexity.

• objective;

• lifecycle;

• risk;

• capability boundary;

• independence;

• consumer;

• evidence;

• decision authority.

36\. Canonical Freeze Statement

B2 — Knowledge & Intelligence Sub Division Architecture establishes the organizational container layer through which PD-04 translates its Knowledge, Context, and Intelligence Assets responsibilities into explicit organizational ownership while preserving Knowledge Authority, Platform autonomy, and cross-platform domain boundaries.

The available source material establishes the PD-04 Knowledge & Intelligence domain and its responsibility relationship to Knowledge, Context, and Intelligence Assets, but does not establish a literal final PD-04 Sub Division structure. Therefore, any concrete Sub Division naming or decomposition in this section is classified as bounded reconstruction rather than historical source transcription.

No Sub Division may become canonical solely because it is architecturally plausible. A final Sub Division requires responsibility, ownership, boundary, output, consumer, interface, and traceability evidence, or an explicit authoritative Architecture Decision.

B2 is FROZEN with explicit source qualification.

4\. Capability Architecture Constitution

CAP-01 — Capability Defines Organizational Ability

Capability menjelaskan kemampuan organisasi untuk menghasilkan outcome secara konsisten.

Capability bukan:

• task;

• checklist;

• job description;

• workflow;

• individual skill.

⸻

CAP-02 — Every Capability Has an Owner

Setiap capability canonical harus memiliki:

• satu primary owner;

• consumer;

• output;

• dependency;

• lifecycle;

• maturity expectation.

⸻

CAP-03 — Capability ≠ Activity

Tidak:

Membuat knowledge entry.

Tetapi:

Knowledge Asset Management Capability

Tidak:

Memperbarui context.

Tetapi:

Context Management Capability

⸻

CAP-04 — Capability ≠ Artifact

Artifact adalah hasil atau objek yang dikelola capability.

Capability

↓

Produces / manages

↓

Artifact

Contoh:

Knowledge Modeling Capability

↓

Knowledge Model

Bukan:

Knowledge Model

\=

Capability

⸻

CAP-05 — Capability ≠ Sub Division

Relationship:

Sub Division

↓

owns

↓

Capability

Sub Division dan capability tidak boleh diperlakukan sebagai sinonim.

⸻

CAP-06 — Domain-Specific Capability

Pattern capability architecture dapat digunakan lintas Platform, tetapi isi capability harus mengikuti domain Platform masing-masing.

Yang diwariskan:

• ownership model;

• capability definition pattern;

• dependency model;

• lifecycle model;

• maturity model.

Yang tidak diwariskan:

• capability names;

• capability catalogue;

• capability count;

• domain outputs.

⸻

CAP-07 — Capability Boundary

Capability PD-04 harus tetap berada dalam:

Knowledge & Intelligence domain.

Capability tidak boleh mengambil ownership:

• Runtime;

• Agent Engineering;

• Infrastructure;

• Security;

• Governance;

• Quality;

• Developer Experience.

⸻

35\. Canonical Freeze Statement

B3 — Knowledge & Intelligence Capability Architecture establishes the capability layer through which PD-04 translates its Knowledge, Context, and Intelligence Assets responsibilities into owned organizational capabilities while preserving Platform autonomy and cross-platform domain boundaries.

The available source establishes the identity, authority, responsibility domains, and major Knowledge & Intelligence artifacts of PD-04. It does not establish a literal PD-04 capability catalogue. Therefore, the capability model in this section is classified as bounded reconstruction derived from explicit PD-04 responsibilities, artifact ownership, and the established AIOS capability architecture pattern.

The reconstructed capabilities must not be treated as historical source facts unless corroborated by an authoritative PD-04 source or Architecture Decision.

Capability ownership remains within PD-04. Consumer relationships do not transfer ownership. Runtime, Agent Engineering, Infrastructure, Governance, Security, Quality, and Developer Experience remain separate Platform domains.

B3 is FROZEN WITH SOURCE QUALIFICATION.

37\. Final Architectural Decision

B3 dinyatakan PASS dan dapat di-freeze.

Namun ada satu guardrail yang sengaja dipertahankan:

Kita tidak mengubah capability reconstruction menjadi source fact hanya karena strukturnya masuk akal.

Itu penting karena source secara eksplisit mendukung PD-04 sebagai Knowledge & Intelligence, Knowledge Authority, serta responsibility Knowledge, Context, Intelligence Assets, dan mendukung ownership Knowledge Base, Knowledge Graph, dan Ontology.

Tetapi daftar capability PD-04 sendiri belum ditemukan secara eksplisit. Maka B3 saat ini adalah Canonical Bounded Reconstruction, bukan historical transcription.

4\. Capability Architecture Constitution

CAP-01 — Capability Defines Organizational Ability

Capability menjelaskan kemampuan organisasi untuk menghasilkan outcome secara konsisten.

Capability bukan:

• task;

• checklist;

• job description;

• workflow;

• individual skill.

⸻

CAP-02 — Every Capability Has an Owner

Setiap capability canonical harus memiliki:

• satu primary owner;

• consumer;

• output;

• dependency;

• lifecycle;

• maturity expectation.

⸻

CAP-03 — Capability ≠ Activity

Tidak:

Membuat knowledge entry.

Tetapi:

Knowledge Asset Management Capability

Tidak:

Memperbarui context.

Tetapi:

Context Management Capability

⸻

CAP-04 — Capability ≠ Artifact

Artifact adalah hasil atau objek yang dikelola capability.

Capability

↓

Produces / manages

↓

Artifact

Contoh:

Knowledge Modeling Capability

↓

Knowledge Model

Bukan:

Knowledge Model

\=

Capability

⸻

CAP-05 — Capability ≠ Sub Division

Relationship:

Sub Division

↓

owns

↓

Capability

Sub Division dan capability tidak boleh diperlakukan sebagai sinonim.

⸻

CAP-06 — Domain-Specific Capability

Pattern capability architecture dapat digunakan lintas Platform, tetapi isi capability harus mengikuti domain Platform masing-masing.

Yang diwariskan:

• ownership model;

• capability definition pattern;

• dependency model;

• lifecycle model;

• maturity model.

Yang tidak diwariskan:

• capability names;

• capability catalogue;

• capability count;

• domain outputs.

⸻

CAP-07 — Capability Boundary

Capability PD-04 harus tetap berada dalam:

Knowledge & Intelligence domain.

Capability tidak boleh mengambil ownership:

• Runtime;

• Agent Engineering;

• Infrastructure;

• Security;

• Governance;

• Quality;

• Developer Experience.

⸻

35\. Canonical Freeze Statement

B3 — Knowledge & Intelligence Capability Architecture establishes the capability layer through which PD-04 translates its Knowledge, Context, and Intelligence Assets responsibilities into owned organizational capabilities while preserving Platform autonomy and cross-platform domain boundaries.

The available source establishes the identity, authority, responsibility domains, and major Knowledge & Intelligence artifacts of PD-04. It does not establish a literal PD-04 capability catalogue. Therefore, the capability model in this section is classified as bounded reconstruction derived from explicit PD-04 responsibilities, artifact ownership, and the established AIOS capability architecture pattern.

The reconstructed capabilities must not be treated as historical source facts unless corroborated by an authoritative PD-04 source or Architecture Decision.

Capability ownership remains within PD-04. Consumer relationships do not transfer ownership. Runtime, Agent Engineering, Infrastructure, Governance, Security, Quality, and Developer Experience remain separate Platform domains.

B3 is FROZEN WITH SOURCE QUALIFICATION.

37\. Final Architectural Decision

B3 dinyatakan PASS dan dapat di-freeze.

Namun ada satu guardrail yang sengaja dipertahankan:

Kita tidak mengubah capability reconstruction menjadi source fact hanya karena strukturnya masuk akal.

Itu penting karena source secara eksplisit mendukung PD-04 sebagai Knowledge & Intelligence, Knowledge Authority, serta responsibility Knowledge, Context, Intelligence Assets, dan mendukung ownership Knowledge Base, Knowledge Graph, dan Ontology.

Tetapi daftar capability PD-04 sendiri belum ditemukan secara eksplisit. Maka B3 saat ini adalah Canonical Bounded Reconstruction, bukan historical transcription.

4\. Team Architecture Constitution

TA-01 — Capability Driven Team Design

Team dibentuk untuk menjalankan capability.

Strategic Mandate

↓

Responsibility

↓

Capability

↓

Team

Team tidak boleh dibentuk hanya karena:

• jumlah pekerjaan;

• preferensi struktur;

• naming convention;

• asumsi organisasi tradisional.

⸻

TA-02 — One Team One Primary Purpose

Setiap Team harus mempunyai satu primary purpose.

Team dapat mendukung capability lain, tetapi tidak boleh memiliki primary ownership yang ambigu.

⸻

TA-03 — One Primary Operational Owner

Setiap Team memiliki satu primary operational owner.

Supporting Teams dapat berkolaborasi.

Namun:

Collaboration does not create multiple primary owners.

⸻

TA-04 — Capability Alignment

Setiap Team harus memiliki capability alignment yang dapat ditelusuri.

Team

↓

Primary Capability

↓

Capability Owner

⸻

TA-05 — Minimum Necessary Structure

Team hanya dibuat apabila organizational separation benar-benar memberikan manfaat material.

Jangan membuat Team terpisah jika:

• boundary belum jelas;

• capability belum cukup material;

• output masih sama;

• ownership tetap ambigu.

⸻

TA-06 — Team ≠ Role Group

Team adalah organizational execution container.

Role Group adalah responsibility cluster di dalam Team.

Team

↓

Role Group

Role Group akan ditentukan pada B5.

⸻

TA-07 — Team ≠ Workflow

Workflow menggambarkan bagaimana pekerjaan berjalan.

Team menjelaskan siapa organizational owner yang menjalankan capability.

Team

≠

Workflow

⸻

TA-08 — Team ≠ AI Workforce

Team adalah organizational container.

AI Workforce adalah workforce implementation layer.

Team

↓

AI Workforce

28\. Team Creation Rule

0\. responsibility material;

0\. primary purpose jelas;

0\. ownership dapat ditentukan;

0\. boundary dapat ditentukan;

0\. output dapat ditentukan;

0\. consumer dapat ditentukan;

0\. dependency dapat dijelaskan;

0\. Team tidak menduplikasi Team lain;

0\. creation tidak melanggar Platform boundary.

29\. Team Merge Rule

Dua Team dapat digabung apabila:

• responsibility overlap material;

• capability coupling tinggi;

• separation tidak lagi diperlukan;

• ownership dapat disederhanakan;

• output dapat dikelola dalam satu boundary.

Merge harus memiliki:

• rationale;

• impact analysis;

• authority;

• transition;

• record.

⸻

30\. Team Split Rule

Team dapat dipecah apabila:

• responsibility terlalu luas;

• capability complexity meningkat;

• independence menjadi material;

• output memiliki consumer yang berbeda;

• ownership conflict muncul;

• lifecycle berbeda secara material.

Split tidak boleh dilakukan hanya karena Team menjadi "terlalu sibuk".

42\. Canonical Freeze Statement

B4 — Knowledge & Intelligence Team Architecture establishes the Team layer through which PD-04 may operationalize its Knowledge, Context, and Intelligence Assets capabilities while preserving Knowledge Authority, capability ownership, and cross-platform domain boundaries.

The available source establishes Team as an organizational layer within the AIOS organization model and establishes PD-04 as Knowledge & Intelligence under Knowledge Authority. However, the available source does not establish a literal PD-04 Team catalogue.

Accordingly, the Team structure in this section is a bounded reconstruction derived from the B3 capability model and the established organizational architecture pattern. It must not be represented as historical canonical fact unless corroborated by an authoritative PD-04 source or Architecture Decision.

No Team may claim ownership of Runtime, AI Engineering, Infrastructure, Quality & Evaluation, Governance & Compliance, or any other Platform domain merely because it has a dependency or consumer relationship with that domain.

B4 is FROZEN WITH SOURCE QUALIFICATION.

44\. Final Architectural Decision

B4 = PASS — APPROVED WITH SOURCE QUALIFICATION.

Keputusan pentingnya adalah kita tidak menganggap "10 capability → 10 Team" sebagai fakta baseline. Itu hanya decomposition yang saat ini paling mudah ditelusuri dari B3.

Justru source yang tersedia memberi guardrail yang sangat jelas:

Internal Structure Must Be Explicitly Established — Sub Division, Capability, Team, dan Role Group tidak boleh diinferensikan sebagai canonical tanpa source/Architecture Decision yang relevan.

Jadi B4 sudah dapat freeze sebagai bounded canonical construction, sementara Claude Code mendapat source investigation directive untuk mencari Team identity yang sebenarnya dari baseline/repository/GitHub.

4\. Team Architecture Constitution

TA-01 — Capability Driven Team Design

Team dibentuk untuk menjalankan capability.

Strategic Mandate

↓

Responsibility

↓

Capability

↓

Team

Team tidak boleh dibentuk hanya karena:

• jumlah pekerjaan;

• preferensi struktur;

• naming convention;

• asumsi organisasi tradisional.

⸻

TA-02 — One Team One Primary Purpose

Setiap Team harus mempunyai satu primary purpose.

Team dapat mendukung capability lain, tetapi tidak boleh memiliki primary ownership yang ambigu.

⸻

TA-03 — One Primary Operational Owner

Setiap Team memiliki satu primary operational owner.

Supporting Teams dapat berkolaborasi.

Namun:

Collaboration does not create multiple primary owners.

⸻

TA-04 — Capability Alignment

Setiap Team harus memiliki capability alignment yang dapat ditelusuri.

Team

↓

Primary Capability

↓

Capability Owner

⸻

TA-05 — Minimum Necessary Structure

Team hanya dibuat apabila organizational separation benar-benar memberikan manfaat material.

Jangan membuat Team terpisah jika:

• boundary belum jelas;

• capability belum cukup material;

• output masih sama;

• ownership tetap ambigu.

⸻

TA-06 — Team ≠ Role Group

Team adalah organizational execution container.

Role Group adalah responsibility cluster di dalam Team.

Team

↓

Role Group

Role Group akan ditentukan pada B5.

⸻

TA-07 — Team ≠ Workflow

Workflow menggambarkan bagaimana pekerjaan berjalan.

Team menjelaskan siapa organizational owner yang menjalankan capability.

Team

≠

Workflow

⸻

TA-08 — Team ≠ AI Workforce

Team adalah organizational container.

AI Workforce adalah workforce implementation layer.

Team

↓

AI Workforce

• responsibility overlap material;

• capability coupling tinggi;

• authority;

• transition;

• record.

30\. Team Split Rule

• responsibility terlalu luas;

• capability complexity meningkat;

• independence menjadi material;

• output memiliki consumer yang berbeda;

• ownership conflict muncul;

• lifecycle berbeda secara material.

Split tidak boleh dilakukan hanya karena Team menjadi "terlalu sibuk".

\- move Quality Platform ownership into PD-04.

3\. reopen B4 if the delta is material;

4\. preserve CPID PD-04;

5\. preserve Knowledge Authority;

6\. preserve Platform boundary;

7\. record Architecture Decision where reconciliation is required.

42\. Canonical Freeze Statement

B4 — Knowledge & Intelligence Team Architecture establishes the Team layer through which PD-04 may operationalize its Knowledge, Context, and Intelligence Assets capabilities while preserving Knowledge Authority, capability ownership, and cross-platform domain boundaries.

The available source establishes Team as an organizational layer within the AIOS organization model and establishes PD-04 as Knowledge & Intelligence under Knowledge Authority. However, the available source does not establish a literal PD-04 Team catalogue.

Accordingly, the Team structure in this section is a bounded reconstruction derived from the B3 capability model and the established organizational architecture pattern. It must not be represented as historical canonical fact unless corroborated by an authoritative PD-04 source or Architecture Decision.

No Team may claim ownership of Runtime, AI Engineering, Infrastructure, Quality & Evaluation, Governance & Compliance, or any other Platform domain merely because it has a dependency or consumer relationship with that domain.

B4 is FROZEN WITH SOURCE QUALIFICATION.

44\. Final Architectural Decision

B4 = PASS — APPROVED WITH SOURCE QUALIFICATION.

Keputusan pentingnya adalah kita tidak menganggap "10 capability → 10 Team" sebagai fakta baseline. Itu hanya decomposition yang saat ini paling mudah ditelusuri dari B3.

Justru source yang tersedia memberi guardrail yang sangat jelas:

Internal Structure Must Be Explicitly Established — Sub Division, Capability, Team, dan Role Group tidak boleh diinferensikan sebagai canonical tanpa source/Architecture Decision yang relevan.

Jadi B4 sudah dapat freeze sebagai bounded canonical construction, sementara Claude Code mendapat source investigation directive untuk mencari Team identity yang sebenarnya dari baseline/repository/GitHub.

4\. Role Group Constitution

RG-01 — Responsibility-Based Design

Role Group dibentuk berdasarkan responsibility cluster.

Role Group bukan daftar jabatan.

⸻

RG-02 — Team-Bounded Design

Setiap Role Group memiliki primary Team.

Team

↓

Role Group

Supporting relationship tidak mengubah primary ownership.

⸻

RG-03 — One Primary Responsibility Cluster

Setiap Role Group memiliki satu primary responsibility cluster.

Role Group dapat memiliki supporting responsibilities selama tidak menciptakan ownership ambiguity.

⸻

RG-04 — Capability Alignment

Setiap Role Group harus dapat ditelusuri kepada capability yang dijalankan Team.

Capability

↓

Team

↓

Role Group

⸻

RG-05 — No Role-First Architecture

Role Group tidak dibangun dari daftar jabatan tradisional.

Urutan yang benar:

Capability

↓

Team

↓

Responsibility Cluster

↓

Role Group

↓

Role

⸻

RG-06 — Human / AI Workforce Compatibility

Role Group harus kompatibel dengan:

• human workforce;

• AI workforce;

• hybrid workforce.

Namun bentuk workforce tidak ditetapkan pada B5.

⸻

RG-07 — Separation of Concern

Role Group harus memiliki boundary yang jelas.

Tidak boleh satu Role Group sekaligus menjadi:

• primary owner;

• independent reviewer;

• consumer;

• execution owner

untuk responsibility yang secara material membutuhkan segregation.

⸻

RG-08 — Minimum Necessary Decomposition

Tidak semua Team harus memiliki banyak Role Group.

Role Group hanya dibuat apabila responsibility cluster cukup material untuk membutuhkan organizational distinction.

⸻

RG-09 — Stable Pattern, Evolving Implementation

Role Group architecture harus stabil sebagai structural pattern.

Namun:

Role Group

↓

Role

↓

Workforce

dapat berkembang tanpa otomatis mengubah Platform identity.

⸻

27\. Role Group Creation Rule

Role Group baru hanya dapat dibentuk apabila:

0\. responsibility cluster material;

0\. primary Team jelas;

0\. capability alignment jelas;

0\. output dapat didefinisikan;

0\. boundary dapat didefinisikan;

0\. consumer dapat diidentifikasi;

0\. ownership tidak duplikatif;

0\. segregation requirement terpenuhi;

0\. creation tidak mengambil domain Platform lain;

0\. creation dapat dipertanggungjawabkan secara architectural.

28\. Role Group Merge Rule

Role Group dapat digabung apabila:

• responsibility overlap;

• separation tidak lagi material;

• output terlalu closely coupled;

• ownership lebih jelas dalam satu cluster;

• governance risk tidak meningkat.

Merge harus memiliki:

• rationale;

• impact;

• authority;

• transition;

• traceability.

⸻

29\. Role Group Split Rule

Role Group dapat dipecah apabila:

• responsibility terlalu luas;

• independence requirement meningkat;

• output berbeda secara material;

• consumer berbeda;

• risk profile berbeda;

• ownership menjadi ambigu.

Split tidak boleh dilakukan hanya karena workload meningkat.

46\. Canonical Freeze Statement

B5 — Knowledge & Intelligence Role Group Architecture establishes the responsibility-cluster layer beneath PD-04 Teams and provides the structural bridge toward human, AI, or hybrid workforce implementation while preserving Knowledge Authority, capability ownership, Team boundaries, and cross-platform domain ownership.

The available source establishes Role Group as an organizational architecture layer beneath Team and establishes the requirement that internal organizational structures be explicitly supported by source or Architecture Decision. The available source does not establish a literal PD-04 Role Group catalogue.

Accordingly, the Role Group structure in this section is a bounded reconstruction derived from the frozen B4 Team Architecture and the established organization architecture pattern. It must not be represented as historical canonical fact until corroborated by an authoritative PD-04 source or Architecture Decision.

Role Group does not create authority. Role Group does not create Platform ownership. Role Group does not create workflow ownership. Role Group does not determine individual human or AI workforce implementation.

B5 is FROZEN WITH SOURCE QUALIFICATION.

48\. Final Architectural Decision

B5 = PASS — APPROVED WITH SOURCE QUALIFICATION.

The important architectural decision is:

Team

↓

Role Group

is confirmed as the correct architecture layer, but:

10 Teams

↓

10 Role Groups

is not treated as a source-mandated rule.

The current B5 model is therefore:

a bounded construction scaffold, not a historical PD-04 organizational fact.

The next section may proceed only within this boundary.

\*\*Catatan penting:\*\* pola \`Capability → Team → Role Group → Workforce Role\` memang didukung sebagai organizational architecture pattern di source, tetapi source yang ditemukan belum memberikan literal PD-04 Role Group catalogue. Performance Architecture Review.txt Jadi keputusan \*\*PASS WITH SOURCE QUALIFICATION\*\* di atas adalah sengaja dipertahankan agar tidak terjadi \*false canonicalization\*.

Kelompok responsibility apa yang diperlukan di dalam setiap Team untuk menjalankan capability PD-04 secara accountable, bounded, dan dapat diteruskan menjadi workforce architecture?

4\. Role Group Constitution

RG-01 — Responsibility-Based Design

Role Group dibentuk berdasarkan responsibility cluster.

Role Group bukan daftar jabatan.

⸻

RG-02 — Team-Bounded Design

Setiap Role Group memiliki primary Team.

Team

↓

Role Group

Supporting relationship tidak mengubah primary ownership.

⸻

RG-03 — One Primary Responsibility Cluster

Setiap Role Group memiliki satu primary responsibility cluster.

Role Group dapat memiliki supporting responsibilities selama tidak menciptakan ownership ambiguity.

⸻

RG-04 — Capability Alignment

Setiap Role Group harus dapat ditelusuri kepada capability yang dijalankan Team.

Capability

↓

Team

↓

Role Group

⸻

RG-05 — No Role-First Architecture

Role Group tidak dibangun dari daftar jabatan tradisional.

Urutan yang benar:

Capability

↓

Team

↓

Responsibility Cluster

↓

Role Group

↓

Role

⸻

RG-06 — Human / AI Workforce Compatibility

Role Group harus kompatibel dengan:

• human workforce;

• AI workforce;

• hybrid workforce.

Namun bentuk workforce tidak ditetapkan pada B5.

⸻

RG-07 — Separation of Concern

Role Group harus memiliki boundary yang jelas.

Tidak boleh satu Role Group sekaligus menjadi:

• primary owner;

• independent reviewer;

• consumer;

• execution owner

untuk responsibility yang secara material membutuhkan segregation.

⸻

RG-08 — Minimum Necessary Decomposition

Tidak semua Team harus memiliki banyak Role Group.

Role Group hanya dibuat apabila responsibility cluster cukup material untuk membutuhkan organizational distinction.

⸻

RG-09 — Stable Pattern, Evolving Implementation

Role Group architecture harus stabil sebagai structural pattern.

Namun:

Role Group

↓

Role

↓

Workforce

dapat berkembang tanpa otomatis mengubah Platform identity.

⸻

6\. Role Group 01 — Knowledge Architecture Role Group

Primary Team

Knowledge Architecture Team

Primary Capability

Knowledge Architecture & Modeling

Purpose

Menjalankan responsibility cluster untuk knowledge architecture, modeling, structure, dan relationship definition.

Responsibilities

• knowledge model development;

• knowledge structure;

• conceptual relationships;

• architecture consistency;

• knowledge model maintenance.

Outputs

• Knowledge Model;

• Knowledge Architecture Specification;

• Knowledge Relationship Definition.

Boundary

Tidak memiliki primary ownership atas:

• Runtime;

• Agent Engineering;

• Infrastructure;

• external execution.

19\. Role Group Boundary Rules

RG-B01 — No Cross-Team Ownership Without Decision

Role Group tidak boleh mengambil primary ownership Team lain tanpa explicit Architecture Decision.

⸻

RG-B02 — Dependency Does Not Create Ownership

Dependency

≠

Ownership

⸻

RG-B03 — Consumer Does Not Become Owner

Pengguna Knowledge Asset tidak otomatis menjadi owner asset.

⸻

RG-B04 — Quality Does Not Become Domain Ownership

Knowledge Quality Role Group dapat menilai integrity.

Ia tidak menjadi owner seluruh Knowledge domain.

⸻

RG-B05 — Evolution Does Not Become Uncontrolled Change

Knowledge Evolution Role Group tidak boleh melakukan perubahan canonical tanpa applicable governance/change authority.

⸻

RG-B06 — Workforce Does Not Redefine Boundary

Human atau AI workforce yang menjalankan Role Group tidak mengubah ownership boundary.

27\. Role Group Creation Rule

Role Group baru hanya dapat dibentuk apabila:

0\. responsibility cluster material;

0\. primary Team jelas;

0\. capability alignment jelas;

0\. output dapat didefinisikan;

0\. boundary dapat didefinisikan;

0\. consumer dapat diidentifikasi;

0\. ownership tidak duplikatif;

0\. segregation requirement terpenuhi;

0\. creation tidak mengambil domain Platform lain;

0\. creation dapat dipertanggungjawabkan secara architectural.

⸻

28\. Role Group Merge Rule

Role Group dapat digabung apabila:

• responsibility overlap;

• separation tidak lagi material;

• output terlalu closely coupled;

• ownership lebih jelas dalam satu cluster;

• governance risk tidak meningkat.

Merge harus memiliki:

• rationale;

• impact;

• authority;

• transition;

• traceability.

⸻

29\. Role Group Split Rule

Role Group dapat dipecah apabila:

• responsibility terlalu luas;

• independence requirement meningkat;

• output berbeda secara material;

• consumer berbeda;

• risk profile berbeda;

• ownership menjadi ambigu.

Split tidak boleh dilakukan hanya karena workload meningkat.

⸻

Role Group maturity dapat dinilai melalui:

• responsibility clarity;

• ownership clarity;

• output reliability;

• boundary integrity;

• dependency management;

• evidence quality;

• workforce readiness;

• improvement capability.

Headcount bukan maturity measure.

AP-01 — Role-First Design

Authority tidak otomatis menghasilkan Role Group.

AP-10 — Historical Overclaim

Reconstructed Role Group tidak boleh dipresentasikan sebagai source-established fact tanpa evidence.

46\. Canonical Freeze Statement

B5 — Knowledge & Intelligence Role Group Architecture establishes the responsibility-cluster layer beneath PD-04 Teams and provides the structural bridge toward human, AI, or hybrid workforce implementation while preserving Knowledge Authority, capability ownership, Team boundaries, and cross-platform domain ownership.

The available source establishes Role Group as an organizational architecture layer beneath Team and establishes the requirement that internal organizational structures be explicitly supported by source or Architecture Decision. The available source does not establish a literal PD-04 Role Group catalogue.

Accordingly, the Role Group structure in this section is a bounded reconstruction derived from the frozen B4 Team Architecture and the established organization architecture pattern. It must not be represented as historical canonical fact until corroborated by an authoritative PD-04 source or Architecture Decision.

Role Group does not create authority. Role Group does not create Platform ownership. Role Group does not create workflow ownership. Role Group does not determine individual human or AI workforce implementation.

B5 is FROZEN WITH SOURCE QUALIFICATION.

Team

a bounded construction scaffold, not a historical PD-04 organizational fact.

\# 1. Purpose

↓

maka B6 menjawab:

Bagaimana organizational accountability, reporting relationship, escalation visibility, dan information flow PD-04 disusun tanpa mengubah domain ownership atau authority boundary?

B6 bukan sekadar daftar "siapa melapor kepada siapa".

B6 menetapkan:

• reporting relationship;

• organizational accountability;

• reporting aggregation;

• escalation visibility;

• management information flow;

• reporting independence;

2\. Source Discovery Result

Reporting Hierarchy merupakan structural layer setelah Role Group Architecture.

Pattern:

Organizational Model

↓

Sub Division

↓

Capability

↓

Team

↓

Role Group

Reporting Hierarchy

Performance Architecture Review.txt

Dengan demikian:

B6 mengoperasionalkan organizational structure yang telah dibangun pada B1–B5; B6 tidak menciptakan organizational structure baru.

• separation antara reporting hierarchy dan authority hierarchy;

• prinsip bahwa dependency tidak berarti subordination.

specific Role Group

dan bukan:

4\. Reporting Constitution

RH-01 — Reporting Does Not Create Ownership

Reporting relationship tidak menciptakan domain ownership baru.

Reporting

≠

Domain Ownership

⸻

RH-02 — Reporting Does Not Create Authority

Atasan dalam reporting line tidak otomatis memperoleh authority atas seluruh domain yang dilaporkan.

Reporting Line

≠

Authority Transfer

⸻

RH-03 — Reporting Follows Organizational Structure

Reporting hierarchy harus diturunkan dari B1–B5.

Tidak boleh:

Reporting Requirement

↓

New Organizational Unit

secara informal.

⸻

RH-04 — One Primary Reporting Path

Setiap organizational unit harus memiliki satu primary reporting path untuk organizational accountability.

Additional relationships dapat berupa:

• functional;

• advisory;

• dotted-line;

• coordination;

• governance;

• review.

Namun primary accountability harus tetap identifiable.

⸻

RH-05 — Functional Relationship Is Not Automatically Reporting

Functional coordination tidak otomatis menjadi reporting relationship.

Functional Dependency

≠

Reporting Relationship

⸻

RH-06 — Cross-Platform Relationship Is Not Reporting

PD-04 dapat menerima input, requirement, review, atau governance requirement dari Platform lain tanpa menjadikan Platform tersebut sebagai reporting parent.

⸻

RH-07 — Independence Must Be Preserved

Jika responsibility membutuhkan independence:

Execution

≠

Independent Review

reporting line harus mendukung independence tersebut.

8\. Layer 1 — Role Group Reporting

• blockers;

Team

• Role Group status;

• improvement actions.

Model:

Role Group A ─┐

Role Group B ─┼──→ Team Report

Role Group C ─┘

Aggregation tidak boleh menghapus evidence penting.

• knowledge quality;

• intelligence asset condition;

• cross-team dependencies;

• material risks;

• improvement portfolio.

memberikan gambaran kesehatan Knowledge & Intelligence domain secara integrated.

• consumer dapat mengubah result;

37\. Reporting Conflict Resolution

Jika terjadi konflik:

Case 1 — Two reporting parents

Tetapkan:

• primary reporting line;

• secondary functional line;

• authority boundary.

Case 2 — Reporting parent owns different domain

Pertahankan domain ownership.

Case 3 — Dotted-line conflict

Primary reporting line menjadi default organizational accountability.

Case 4 — Governance reporting conflict

Governance requirement tidak otomatis mengubah organizational reporting line.

Case 5 — Independent review conflict

Independence requirement memiliki precedence untuk review relationship yang relevan.

⸻

B6 tidak menetapkan:

• individu;

• headcount;

• job title;

• compensation;

• recruitment.

B6 hanya menetapkan reporting relationship.

Model:

Organization

↓

Reporting Hierarchy

↓

Role Group

↓

Workforce

Human Workforce

AI Workforce

Hybrid Workforce

Authority tetap berasal dari applicable authority architecture.

40\. Anti-Pattern Rules

AP-01 — Reporting-as-Ownership

Jangan menganggap siapa yang menerima report sebagai owner.

⸻

AP-02 — Reporting-as-Authority

Jangan memberikan decision authority hanya karena seseorang berada di atas reporting line.

⸻

AP-03 — Cross-Platform Reporting Takeover

Jangan menjadikan PD-02/PD-03/PD-05/PD-06/PD-07/PD-08/PD-09/PD-10 sebagai reporting parent PD-04 tanpa explicit organizational decision.

⸻

AP-04 — Dotted-Line Ambiguity

Jangan membuat multiple reporting lines tanpa primary accountability.

⸻

AP-05 — Governance Takeover

Governance reporting tidak otomatis menjadi organizational reporting.

⸻

AP-06 — Consumer-as-Manager

Consumer tidak otomatis menjadi manager.

⸻

AP-07 — Aggregation Hiding

Jangan menghapus material exception melalui aggregation.

⸻

AP-08 — Informal Boundary Change

Jangan mengubah reporting hierarchy untuk secara diam-diam mengubah organizational ownership.

47\. Canonical Freeze Statement

B6 — Knowledge & Intelligence Reporting Hierarchy establishes the reporting and organizational accountability layer for PD-04 beneath the frozen Organization Architecture while preserving Knowledge Authority, domain ownership, organizational boundaries, independence requirements, and cross-platform autonomy.

Reporting hierarchy is not authority hierarchy. Reporting relationship is not ownership transfer. Consumer relationship is not organizational subordination. Governance relationship is not automatically a reporting relationship.

The available source establishes Reporting Hierarchy as an Organization Architecture layer but does not establish a literal PD-04 reporting tree. Therefore the current PD-04 reporting model is a bounded reconstruction derived from B1–B5 and must not be represented as historical canonical fact until corroborated by an authoritative source or Architecture Decision.

B6 is FROZEN WITH SOURCE QUALIFICATION.

\# 1. Purpose

B6 menetapkan arsitektur reporting hierarchy untuk PD-04 Knowledge & Intelligence.

Organizational Model

Sub Division Architecture

↓

↓

Team Architecture

↓

Reporting Hierarchy

maka B6 menjawab:

Bagaimana organizational accountability, reporting relationship, escalation visibility, dan information flow PD-04 disusun tanpa mengubah domain ownership atau authority boundary?

B6 bukan sekadar daftar "siapa melapor kepada siapa".

B6 menetapkan:

• reporting relationship;

• organizational accountability;

• reporting aggregation;

• escalation visibility;

• management information flow;

• reporting boundary;

• reporting independence;

• reporting change principles.

Reporting Hierarchy merupakan structural layer setelah Role Group Architecture.

Organizational Model

↓

Sub Division

↓

Capability

↓

Team

↓

Role Group

↓

Reporting Hierarchy

Performance Architecture Review.txt

Dengan demikian:

B6 mengoperasionalkan organizational structure yang telah dibangun pada B1–B5; B6 tidak menciptakan organizational structure baru.

• Organization Architecture sebagai structural layer;

• Reporting Hierarchy sebagai layer setelah Role Group;

• organizational accountability;

• separation antara reporting hierarchy dan authority hierarchy;

• prinsip bahwa dependency tidak berarti subordination.

Namun source yang tersedia tidak menetapkan literal PD-04 reporting tree.

specific Sub Division Head

specific Team Lead

↓

specific Role Group

\=

Bounded Canonical Reconstruction

dan bukan:

Historical Canonical Fact

4\. Reporting Constitution

RH-01 — Reporting Does Not Create Ownership

Reporting relationship tidak menciptakan domain ownership baru.

Reporting

≠

Domain Ownership

⸻

RH-02 — Reporting Does Not Create Authority

Atasan dalam reporting line tidak otomatis memperoleh authority atas seluruh domain yang dilaporkan.

Reporting Line

≠

Authority Transfer

⸻

RH-03 — Reporting Follows Organizational Structure

Reporting hierarchy harus diturunkan dari B1–B5.

Tidak boleh:

Reporting Requirement

↓

New Organizational Unit

secara informal.

⸻

RH-04 — One Primary Reporting Path

Setiap organizational unit harus memiliki satu primary reporting path untuk organizational accountability.

Additional relationships dapat berupa:

• functional;

• advisory;

• dotted-line;

• coordination;

• governance;

• review.

Namun primary accountability harus tetap identifiable.

⸻

RH-05 — Functional Relationship Is Not Automatically Reporting

Functional coordination tidak otomatis menjadi reporting relationship.

Functional Dependency

≠

Reporting Relationship

⸻

RH-06 — Cross-Platform Relationship Is Not Reporting

PD-04 dapat menerima input, requirement, review, atau governance requirement dari Platform lain tanpa menjadikan Platform tersebut sebagai reporting parent.

⸻

RH-07 — Independence Must Be Preserved

Jika responsibility membutuhkan independence:

Execution

≠

Independent Review

reporting line harus mendukung independence tersebut.

8\. Layer 1 — Role Group Reporting

Role Group reporting berfokus pada:

• responsibility status;

• work status;

• output status;

• evidence;

• blockers;

• dependency;

• risk;

• improvement signal.

Contoh:

Knowledge Quality Role Group

↓

Quality Findings

↓

Team

Role Group tidak melaporkan strategic direction secara langsung sebagai primary organizational responsibility kecuali mekanisme tertentu menetapkannya.

⸻

9\. Layer 2 — Team Reporting

Team mengagregasikan:

• Role Group status;

• capability delivery;

• team workload;

• dependencies;

• material risks;

• quality;

• delivery condition;

• improvement actions.

Model:

Role Group A ─┐

Role Group B ─┼──→ Team Report

Role Group C ─┘

Team report harus mempertahankan material exceptions.

Aggregation tidak boleh menghapus evidence penting.

⸻

10\. Layer 3 — Knowledge Organizational Reporting

Knowledge organizational reporting mengagregasikan:

• capability condition;

• knowledge asset health;

• semantic integrity;

• repository condition;

• knowledge quality;

• context condition;

• intelligence asset condition;

• cross-team dependencies;

• material risks;

• improvement portfolio.

Tujuannya:

memberikan gambaran kesehatan Knowledge & Intelligence domain secara integrated.

23\. Dotted-Line Relationship

Dotted-line reporting dapat digunakan apabila:

• authority boundary;

• escalation rule;

25\. Reporting Accountability

Reporting accountability berarti:

memastikan informasi yang wajib dilaporkan tersedia, benar konteksnya, dan sampai kepada consumer yang tepat.

Reporting accountability tidak berarti:

• consumer memiliki domain;

• consumer memiliki source data;

• consumer dapat mengubah result;

• consumer dapat mengubah organizational structure.

36\. Reporting Change Governance

Reporting hierarchy dapat berubah apabila:

• organizational structure berubah;

• Team berubah;

• Role Group berubah;

• responsibility berubah;

• material independence requirement berubah;

• platform scale berubah.

Change sequence:

Structural Change

↓

Impact Assessment

↓

Reporting Impact

↓

Authority Review

↓

Approved Change

↓

Reporting Hierarchy Update

Reporting hierarchy tidak boleh menjadi tempat pertama untuk menyelesaikan structural conflict.

⸻

37\. Reporting Conflict Resolution

Jika terjadi konflik:

Case 1 — Two reporting parents

Tetapkan:

• primary reporting line;

• secondary functional line;

• authority boundary.

Case 2 — Reporting parent owns different domain

Pertahankan domain ownership.

Case 3 — Dotted-line conflict

Primary reporting line menjadi default organizational accountability.

Case 4 — Governance reporting conflict

Governance requirement tidak otomatis mengubah organizational reporting line.

Case 5 — Independent review conflict

Independence requirement memiliki precedence untuk review relationship yang relevan.

⸻

38\. Reporting and Workforce

B6 tidak menetapkan:

• individu;

• headcount;

• job title;

• compensation;

• recruitment.

B6 hanya menetapkan reporting relationship.

Model:

Organization

↓

Reporting Hierarchy

↓

Role Group

↓

Workforce

Workforce implementation harus mengikuti reporting hierarchy yang telah disetujui.

⸻

39\. Human / AI Workforce Compatibility

Reporting hierarchy harus kompatibel dengan:

Human Workforce

AI Workforce

Hybrid Workforce

AI Agent tidak otomatis menjadi reporting parent hanya karena memiliki autonomy lebih tinggi.

Authority tetap berasal dari applicable authority architecture.

⸻

40\. Anti-Pattern Rules

AP-01 — Reporting-as-Ownership

Jangan menganggap siapa yang menerima report sebagai owner.

⸻

AP-02 — Reporting-as-Authority

Jangan memberikan decision authority hanya karena seseorang berada di atas reporting line.

⸻

AP-03 — Cross-Platform Reporting Takeover

Jangan menjadikan PD-02/PD-03/PD-05/PD-06/PD-07/PD-08/PD-09/PD-10 sebagai reporting parent PD-04 tanpa explicit organizational decision.

⸻

AP-04 — Dotted-Line Ambiguity

Jangan membuat multiple reporting lines tanpa primary accountability.

⸻

AP-05 — Governance Takeover

Governance reporting tidak otomatis menjadi organizational reporting.

⸻

AP-06 — Consumer-as-Manager

Consumer tidak otomatis menjadi manager.

⸻

AP-07 — Aggregation Hiding

Jangan menghapus material exception melalui aggregation.

⸻

AP-08 — Informal Boundary Change

Jangan mengubah reporting hierarchy untuk secara diam-diam mengubah organizational ownership.

⸻

47\. Canonical Freeze Statement

B6 — Knowledge & Intelligence Reporting Hierarchy establishes the reporting and organizational accountability layer for PD-04 beneath the frozen Organization Architecture while preserving Knowledge Authority, domain ownership, organizational boundaries, independence requirements, and cross-platform autonomy.

Reporting hierarchy is not authority hierarchy. Reporting relationship is not ownership transfer. Consumer relationship is not organizational subordination. Governance relationship is not automatically a reporting relationship.

The available source establishes Reporting Hierarchy as an Organization Architecture layer but does not establish a literal PD-04 reporting tree. Therefore the current PD-04 reporting model is a bounded reconstruction derived from B1–B5 and must not be represented as historical canonical fact until corroborated by an authoritative source or Architecture Decision.

B6 is FROZEN WITH SOURCE QUALIFICATION.

\# 1. Purpose

B7 menetapkan arsitektur interface organisasi PD-04 Knowledge & Intelligence dengan:

\- Platform Division lain;

\- internal organizational units;

\- artifact consumers;

\- governance interfaces;

\- architecture interfaces;

\- operational interfaces;

\- quality interfaces;

\- security interfaces;

\- engineering interfaces;

\- infrastructure interfaces.

B7 menjawab:

\> \*\*Bagaimana PD-04 berinteraksi dengan organisasi dan Platform Division lain tanpa mengaburkan ownership, authority, reporting hierarchy, atau domain boundary?\*\*

B7 menjadi jembatan antara:

\`\`\`text

B1 — Organizational Model

↓

B2 — Sub Division Architecture

↓

B3 — Capability Architecture

↓

B4 — Team Architecture

↓

B5 — Role Group Architecture

↓

B6 — Reporting Hierarchy

↓

B7 — Organizational Interface Architecture

B7 tidak menciptakan organizational ownership baru.

⸻

2\. Source Foundation

Baseline AIOS menetapkan bahwa seluruh komunikasi lintas Platform Division wajib melalui Interface Contract.

Canonical examples include:

Knowledge → Runtime

Knowledge Service Contract

Knowledge → AI Engineering

Knowledge Access Contract

Baseline juga menetapkan bahwa Platform Division lain dapat menggunakan artifact milik suatu Platform tanpa menjadi owner artifact tersebut. Owner mempertahankan responsibility atas lifecycle, quality, version, governance, approval, modification, versioning, dan retirement.

Dengan demikian:

Provider

↓

Interface Contract

↓

Consumer

dan bukan:

Provider

↓

Consumer

↓

Ownership Transfer

⸻

Source secara eksplisit mendukung:

• Platform Interface Registry;

• Interface Contract;

• Knowledge ownership;

• Knowledge Base ownership;

• Knowledge Graph ownership;

• Ontology ownership;

• Knowledge → Runtime interface;

• Knowledge → AI Engineering interface;

• distinction antara hierarchy dan dependency;

• consumer principle;

• owner authority atas artifact lifecycle.

Namun source tidak memberikan B7 sebagai section literal dengan seluruh organizational interface matrix PD-04.

Karena itu:

B7

\=

Bounded Canonical Reconstruction

bukan:

Historical Canonical Fact

4\. Fundamental Interface Principles

IF-01 — Interface Does Not Transfer Ownership

Interface relationship tidak memindahkan ownership.

Interface

≠

Ownership Transfer

⸻

IF-02 — Interface Does Not Create Reporting Line

Consumer tidak menjadi reporting parent provider.

Interface

≠

Reporting Hierarchy

⸻

IF-03 — Dependency Does Not Mean Subordination

Platform yang bergantung pada Knowledge tidak menjadi subordinate organizational unit PD-04.

Dependency

≠

Subordination

Baseline secara eksplisit membedakan organizational hierarchy dari platform dependency.

Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

⸻

IF-04 — Provider Owns the Interface

Provider bertanggung jawab terhadap:

• interface definition;

• interface contract;

• version;

• lifecycle;

• compatibility;

• change governance;

• deprecation;

• retirement.

⸻

IF-05 — Consumer Owns Consumption

Consumer bertanggung jawab terhadap:

• valid consumption;

• local integration;

• local implementation;

• local handling;

• local error management;

• compliance terhadap contract.

⸻

IF-06 — Interface Contract Is Canonical Boundary

Cross-platform communication harus memiliki contract yang jelas.

Minimal:

Provider

Consumer

Purpose

Scope

Input

Output

Authority

Version

Compatibility

Security

Governance

Failure

Change

Deprecation

PD-04

Knowledge & Intelligence

│

Provider Interface

PD-04 menyediakan Knowledge / Intelligence capability atau artifact kepada consumer.

Consumer Interface

PD-04 menggunakan capability, policy, architecture, infrastructure, security, quality, atau runtime service dari Platform lain.

Artifact Owner

23\. Interface Contract Minimum

Every material PD-04 external interface should define:

23.1 Provider

Who owns the supplied capability or artifact?

23.2 Consumer

Who consumes it?

23.3 Purpose

Why does the interface exist?

23.4 Scope

What is included?

23.5 Input

What does the provider receive?

23.6 Output

What does the provider expose?

23.7 Authority

Who can approve changes?

23.8 Version

What version is active?

23.9 Compatibility

What compatibility guarantee exists?

23.10 Security

What access constraints apply?

23.11 Governance

Which governance requirements apply?

23.12 Failure

How are failures handled?

23.13 Change

How is the interface modified?

23.14 Deprecation

How is the interface retired?

⸻

24\. Interface Versioning

Every material interface should be versioned.

Example:

Knowledge Service Contract v1.0

Knowledge Service Contract v1.1

Knowledge Service Contract v2.0

Version change classification:

Minor

↓

Backward Compatible

Major

↓

Potentially Breaking

Actual versioning policy must follow applicable engineering and governance standards.

⸻

25\. Backward Compatibility

Where technically feasible:

Interface changes should preserve backward compatibility.

Breaking changes require:

• impact analysis;

• consumer identification;

• migration plan;

• approval;

• version transition;

• deprecation period where applicable.

⸻

↓

↓

↓

↓

Approval

↓

Version Update

↓

Migration

↓

⸻

27\. Interface Dependency Rules

PD-04 interfaces must preserve dependency direction.

AIOS baseline establishes a layered dependency principle in which higher abstraction layers may depend on lower/foundational capabilities, while lower layers must not depend upward on higher abstraction or governance/cognitive layers.

Therefore:

Interface

↓

Must Respect Dependency Boundary

An interface must not be used to circumvent architectural dependency rules.

⸻

Interface access does not create authority over provider.

PD-06

uses Knowledge

↓

PD-06

does not obtain Knowledge Authority

Similarly:

PD-04

uses Runtime

↓

PD-04

does not obtain Runtime Authority

Governance does not become technical implementation owner.

Consumer

↓

Provider

-

36\. Interface Conflict Resolution

Case 1 — Contract Ambiguity

Resolve through provider-owned contract clarification.

Case 2 — Ownership Dispute

Resolve through explicit ownership decision.

Case 3 — Authority Dispute

Resolve through applicable authority architecture.

Case 4 — Architecture Conflict

Escalate to Architecture authority.

Case 5 — Governance Conflict

Escalate to Governance authority.

Case 6 — Security Conflict

Apply applicable Security authority and controls.

Case 7 — Quality Conflict

Apply applicable Quality evaluation authority.

37\. Interface Failure Handling

Interface failures must distinguish:

Contract Failure

↓

Provider Defect

↓

Consumer Misuse

↓

Dependency Failure

↓

Security Failure

↓

Infrastructure Failure

Do not classify all interface failures as provider failures.

⸻

38\. Interface Observability

Material interfaces should expose sufficient observability for:

• availability;

• request status;

• error;

• latency where applicable;

• version;

• dependency;

• contract status;

• material incident.

Observability data should preserve:

• timestamp;

• source;

• version;

• context;

• evidence.

⸻

39\. Interface Security Boundary

Access to PD-04 interfaces must follow:

Identity

-

Authorization

-

Purpose

-

Classification

-

Governance

Public availability of an interface does not imply unrestricted access to Knowledge assets.

⸻

40\. Interface Data Boundary

Interface contract must define:

• what data may cross;

• what metadata crosses;

• what transformations are permitted;

• what data remains internal;

• what data must not cross;

• retention responsibility;

• classification.

Example:

Ontology

↓

PD-04 Semantic Ownership

Consumer may interpret according to contract.

Consumer may not silently redefine canonical semantics.

Semantic change must follow applicable Knowledge governance.

• graph scope;

• semantic version;

• access;

• provenance;

• error semantics.

• corpus scope;

• source;

• access;

• lifecycle.

Consumer may consume authorized Knowledge.

Consumer does not become Knowledge Base owner.

• canonical terminology;

• semantic identity;

• version;

• compatibility;

• change impact.

Ontology change must be treated as potentially cross-platform-impacting.

47\. Interface Success Criteria

B7 is successful when:

0\. material PD-04 interfaces are identifiable;

0\. provider and consumer are explicit;

0\. ownership remains explicit;

0\. interface contract exists where required;

0\. reporting hierarchy is not altered by interface;

0\. authority boundaries remain intact;

0\. dependency direction remains valid;

0\. artifact ownership remains with PD-04;

0\. consumer rights are contract-bounded;

0\. interface version is identifiable;

0\. change process is defined;

0\. security boundary is defined;

0\. governance boundary is defined;

0\. architecture boundary is defined;

0\. quality relationship is defined;

0\. material failures can be escalated;

0\. interface changes are traceable;

0\. semantic authority remains protected.

⸻

48\. Anti-Pattern Rules

AP-01 — Consumer Ownership

Do not infer ownership from usage.

AP-02 — Dependency Subordination

Do not infer subordination from dependency.

AP-03 — Interface Reporting

Do not infer reporting hierarchy from interface.

AP-04 — Interface Authority

Do not infer authority from access.

AP-05 — Governance Takeover

Do not allow governance relationship to silently become technical ownership.

AP-06 — Architecture Takeover

Do not allow architecture interface to silently become Knowledge ownership.

AP-07 — Semantic Takeover

Do not allow consumers to silently redefine canonical Knowledge semantics.

AP-08 — Contractless Critical Interface

Do not operate a material cross-platform interface indefinitely without an identifiable contract.

AP-09 — Hidden Breaking Change

Do not introduce material breaking changes without impact assessment and version/change governance.

57\. Canonical Freeze Statement

B7 — Knowledge & Intelligence Organizational Interface Architecture establishes how PD-04 exchanges capability, artifact, information, governance context, architecture context, and dependency relationships with internal organizational units and other AIOS Platform Divisions while preserving explicit ownership, authority, reporting, security, quality, and domain boundaries.

An interface does not transfer ownership. A dependency does not create subordination. A consumer does not become an owner. Interface access does not create authority. An interface relationship does not create a reporting line.

PD-04 remains the primary organizational owner of Knowledge & Intelligence and of its canonical Knowledge artifacts. Runtime, AI Engineering, Architecture, Governance, Infrastructure, Security, Quality, and Developer Experience retain ownership of their respective domains.

The source establishes the Platform Interface Registry and explicitly supports the Knowledge Service Contract with Runtime and Knowledge Access Contract with AI Engineering. The complete PD-04 B7 organizational interface architecture is not established as a literal source section and therefore remains explicitly classified as Bounded Canonical Reconstruction.

B7 is FROZEN WITH SOURCE QUALIFICATION.

\# 1. Purpose

B7 menetapkan arsitektur interface organisasi PD-04 Knowledge & Intelligence dengan:

\- Platform Division lain;

\- internal organizational units;

\- artifact consumers;

\- governance interfaces;

\- architecture interfaces;

\- operational interfaces;

\- quality interfaces;

\- security interfaces;

\- engineering interfaces;

\- infrastructure interfaces.

B7 menjawab:

\> \*\*Bagaimana PD-04 berinteraksi dengan organisasi dan Platform Division lain tanpa mengaburkan ownership, authority, reporting hierarchy, atau domain boundary?\*\*

B7 menjadi jembatan antara:

\`\`\`text

B1 — Organizational Model

↓

B2 — Sub Division Architecture

↓

B3 — Capability Architecture

↓

B4 — Team Architecture

↓

B5 — Role Group Architecture

↓

B6 — Reporting Hierarchy

↓

B7 — Organizational Interface Architecture

B7 tidak menciptakan organizational ownership baru.

⸻

2\. Source Foundation

Baseline AIOS menetapkan bahwa seluruh komunikasi lintas Platform Division wajib melalui Interface Contract.

Canonical examples include:

Knowledge → Runtime

Knowledge Service Contract

Knowledge → AI Engineering

Knowledge Access Contract

Baseline juga menetapkan bahwa Platform Division lain dapat menggunakan artifact milik suatu Platform tanpa menjadi owner artifact tersebut. Owner mempertahankan responsibility atas lifecycle, quality, version, governance, approval, modification, versioning, dan retirement.

Dengan demikian:

Provider

↓

Interface Contract

↓

Consumer

dan bukan:

Provider

↓

Consumer

↓

Ownership Transfer

4\. Fundamental Interface Principles

IF-01 — Interface Does Not Transfer Ownership

Interface relationship tidak memindahkan ownership.

Interface

≠

Ownership Transfer

⸻

IF-02 — Interface Does Not Create Reporting Line

Consumer tidak menjadi reporting parent provider.

Interface

≠

Reporting Hierarchy

⸻

IF-03 — Dependency Does Not Mean Subordination

Platform yang bergantung pada Knowledge tidak menjadi subordinate organizational unit PD-04.

Dependency

≠

Subordination

Baseline secara eksplisit membedakan organizational hierarchy dari platform dependency.

Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

⸻

IF-04 — Provider Owns the Interface

Provider bertanggung jawab terhadap:

• interface definition;

• interface contract;

• version;

• lifecycle;

• compatibility;

• change governance;

• deprecation;

• retirement.

⸻

IF-05 — Consumer Owns Consumption

Consumer bertanggung jawab terhadap:

• valid consumption;

• local integration;

• local implementation;

• local handling;

• local error management;

• compliance terhadap contract.

⸻

IF-06 — Interface Contract Is Canonical Boundary

Cross-platform communication harus memiliki contract yang jelas.

Minimal:

Provider

Consumer

Purpose

Scope

Input

Output

Authority

Version

Compatibility

Security

Governance

Failure

Change

Deprecation

23\. Interface Contract Minimum

Every material PD-04 external interface should define:

23.1 Provider

Who owns the supplied capability or artifact?

23.2 Consumer

Who consumes it?

23.3 Purpose

Why does the interface exist?

23.4 Scope

What is included?

23.5 Input

What does the provider receive?

23.6 Output

What does the provider expose?

23.7 Authority

Who can approve changes?

23.8 Version

What version is active?

23.9 Compatibility

What compatibility guarantee exists?

23.10 Security

What access constraints apply?

23.11 Governance

Which governance requirements apply?

23.12 Failure

How are failures handled?

23.13 Change

How is the interface modified?

23.14 Deprecation

How is the interface retired?

⸻

24\. Interface Versioning

Every material interface should be versioned.

Example:

Knowledge Service Contract v1.0

Knowledge Service Contract v1.1

Knowledge Service Contract v2.0

Version change classification:

Minor

↓

Backward Compatible

Major

↓

Potentially Breaking

Actual versioning policy must follow applicable engineering and governance standards.

⸻

25\. Backward Compatibility

Where technically feasible:

Interface changes should preserve backward compatibility.

Breaking changes require:

• impact analysis;

• consumer identification;

• migration plan;

• approval;

• version transition;

• deprecation period where applicable.

⸻

⸻

27\. Interface Dependency Rules

PD-04 interfaces must preserve dependency direction.

AIOS baseline establishes a layered dependency principle in which higher abstraction layers may depend on lower/foundational capabilities, while lower layers must not depend upward on higher abstraction or governance/cognitive layers.

Therefore:

Interface

↓

Must Respect Dependency Boundary

An interface must not be used to circumvent architectural dependency rules.

⸻

28\. Interface and Authority

Interface access does not create authority over provider.

Example:

PD-06

uses Knowledge

↓

PD-06

does not obtain Knowledge Authority

Similarly:

PD-04

uses Runtime

↓

PD-04

does not obtain Runtime Authority

⸻

29\. Interface and Reporting

Interface consumers may receive:

• reports;

• status;

• evidence;

• alerts;

• metrics.

This does not create reporting hierarchy.

Report Consumer

≠

Reporting Manager

B6 remains authoritative for organizational reporting.

⸻

30\. Interface and Ownership

Interface relationship must preserve:

One Primary Owner

-

Multiple Authorized Consumers

Example:

Knowledge Graph

│

├── Runtime

├── AI Engineering

├── Quality

└── Other Authorized Consumers

Owner:

PD-04

⸻

31\. Interface and Governance

All material interfaces must remain subject to applicable governance.

Governance can establish:

• minimum controls;

• required documentation;

• approval;

• compliance;

• auditability;

• traceability.

Governance does not become technical implementation owner.

⸻

32\. Interface and Architecture

Architecture may establish:

• interface boundary;

• contract pattern;

• compatibility requirements;

• dependency constraints;

• reference architecture;

• architectural standards.

Architecture does not become Knowledge artifact owner.

⸻

33\. Interface and Security

Security may establish:

• authentication;

• authorization;

• access control;

• classification;

• audit;

• protection.

Security does not become Knowledge semantic owner.

⸻

34\. Interface and Quality

Quality may establish:

• verification;

• evaluation;

• acceptance;

• quality evidence;

• test requirements.

Quality does not become Knowledge owner.

⸻

35\. Interface Escalation

When an interface fails:

Consumer

↓

Provider

↓

Interface Owner

↓

Applicable Domain Authority

If cross-platform conflict exists:

Provider

-

Consumer

↓

Joint Impact Assessment

↓

Applicable Architecture / Governance / Authority

Escalation does not automatically transfer ownership.

⸻

36\. Interface Conflict Resolution

Case 1 — Contract Ambiguity

Resolve through provider-owned contract clarification.

Case 2 — Ownership Dispute

Resolve through explicit ownership decision.

Case 3 — Authority Dispute

Resolve through applicable authority architecture.

Case 4 — Architecture Conflict

Escalate to Architecture authority.

Case 5 — Governance Conflict

Escalate to Governance authority.

Case 6 — Security Conflict

Apply applicable Security authority and controls.

Case 7 — Quality Conflict

Apply applicable Quality evaluation authority.

⸻

37\. Interface Failure Handling

Interface failures must distinguish:

Contract Failure

↓

Provider Defect

↓

Consumer Misuse

↓

Dependency Failure

↓

Security Failure

↓

Infrastructure Failure

Do not classify all interface failures as provider failures.

⸻

38\. Interface Observability

Material interfaces should expose sufficient observability for:

• availability;

• request status;

• error;

• latency where applicable;

• version;

• dependency;

• contract status;

• material incident.

Observability data should preserve:

• timestamp;

• source;

• version;

• context;

• evidence.

⸻

39\. Interface Security Boundary

Access to PD-04 interfaces must follow:

Identity

-

Authorization

-

Purpose

-

Classification

-

Governance

Public availability of an interface does not imply unrestricted access to Knowledge assets.

⸻

40\. Interface Data Boundary

Interface contract must define:

• what data may cross;

• what metadata crosses;

• what transformations are permitted;

• what data remains internal;

• what data must not cross;

• retention responsibility;

• classification.

⸻

41\. Semantic Boundary

PD-04 must preserve semantic authority over Knowledge artifacts.

Example:

Ontology

↓

PD-04 Semantic Ownership

Consumer may interpret according to contract.

Consumer may not silently redefine canonical semantics.

Semantic change must follow applicable Knowledge governance.

⸻

42\. Knowledge Graph Interface

Knowledge Graph interfaces should define:

• graph scope;

• query contract;

• semantic version;

• access;

• provenance;

• freshness;

• consistency expectations;

• error semantics.

Ownership remains PD-04.

⸻

43\. Knowledge Base Interface

Knowledge Base interfaces should define:

• corpus scope;

• source;

• version;

• retrieval contract;

• freshness;

• provenance;

• access;

• lifecycle.

Consumer may consume authorized Knowledge.

Consumer does not become Knowledge Base owner.

⸻

44\. Ontology Interface

Ontology interface should preserve:

• canonical terminology;

• semantic identity;

• version;

• relationship definition;

• compatibility;

• change impact.

Ontology change must be treated as potentially cross-platform-impacting.

⸻

Interface lifecycle ownership remains with the provider unless an explicit governance decision establishes otherwise.

Level 0

Undefined

Level 1

Documented

Contracted

Versioned

47\. Interface Success Criteria

B7 is successful when:

0\. material PD-04 interfaces are identifiable;

0\. provider and consumer are explicit;

0\. ownership remains explicit;

0\. interface contract exists where required;

0\. reporting hierarchy is not altered by interface;

0\. authority boundaries remain intact;

0\. dependency direction remains valid;

0\. artifact ownership remains with PD-04;

0\. consumer rights are contract-bounded;

0\. interface version is identifiable;

0\. change process is defined;

0\. security boundary is defined;

0\. governance boundary is defined;

0\. architecture boundary is defined;

0\. quality relationship is defined;

0\. material failures can be escalated;

0\. interface changes are traceable;

0\. semantic authority remains protected.

⸻

48\. Anti-Pattern Rules

AP-01 — Consumer Ownership

Do not infer ownership from usage.

AP-02 — Dependency Subordination

Do not infer subordination from dependency.

AP-03 — Interface Reporting

Do not infer reporting hierarchy from interface.

AP-04 — Interface Authority

Do not infer authority from access.

AP-05 — Governance Takeover

Do not allow governance relationship to silently become technical ownership.

AP-06 — Architecture Takeover

Do not allow architecture interface to silently become Knowledge ownership.

AP-07 — Semantic Takeover

Do not allow consumers to silently redefine canonical Knowledge semantics.

AP-08 — Contractless Critical Interface

Do not operate a material cross-platform interface indefinitely without an identifiable contract.

AP-09 — Hidden Breaking Change

Do not introduce material breaking changes without impact assessment and version/change governance.

57\. Canonical Freeze Statement

B7 — Knowledge & Intelligence Organizational Interface Architecture establishes how PD-04 exchanges capability, artifact, information, governance context, architecture context, and dependency relationships with internal organizational units and other AIOS Platform Divisions while preserving explicit ownership, authority, reporting, security, quality, and domain boundaries.

An interface does not transfer ownership. A dependency does not create subordination. A consumer does not become an owner. Interface access does not create authority. An interface relationship does not create a reporting line.

PD-04 remains the primary organizational owner of Knowledge & Intelligence and of its canonical Knowledge artifacts. Runtime, AI Engineering, Architecture, Governance, Infrastructure, Security, Quality, and Developer Experience retain ownership of their respective domains.

The source establishes the Platform Interface Registry and explicitly supports the Knowledge Service Contract with Runtime and Knowledge Access Contract with AI Engineering. The complete PD-04 B7 organizational interface architecture is not established as a literal source section and therefore remains explicitly classified as Bounded Canonical Reconstruction.

B7 is FROZEN WITH SOURCE QUALIFICATION.

\# 1. Purpose

B8 menetapkan bagaimana organisasi PD-04 Knowledge & Intelligence melakukan koordinasi:

\- antar Sub Division;

\- antar Capability;

\- antar Team;

\- antar Role Group;

\- dengan Platform Division lain;

\- dengan Architecture;

\- dengan Governance;

\- dengan Runtime;

\- dengan AI Engineering;

\- dengan Infrastructure;

\- dengan Security;

\- dengan Quality;

\- dengan Developer Experience;

\- dan dengan Executive / strategic coordination layer apabila diperlukan.

B8 menjawab pertanyaan:

\> \*\*Bagaimana seluruh unit organisasi PD-04 berkoordinasi untuk menghasilkan Knowledge & Intelligence outcome bersama tanpa menciptakan ownership ganda, authority informal, reporting line tersembunyi, atau dependency yang melanggar boundary?\*\*

B8 melanjutkan B7.

B6

Reporting Hierarchy

↓

B7

Organizational Interface Architecture

↓

B8

Organizational Coordination Architecture

B7 menentukan boundary interface.

B8 menentukan mekanisme koordinasi dalam boundary tersebut.

4\. Coordination Constitution

PD-04 coordination mengikuti prinsip:

Ownership

↓

Responsibility

↓

Capability

↓

Team

↓

Role Group

↓

Coordination

Coordination berada di atas execution interaction, tetapi tidak menggantikan ownership.

⸻

5\. Coordination Principles

CP-01 — Ownership First

Setiap material responsibility harus memiliki primary owner.

Coordination tidak menciptakan ownership kedua.

Collaboration

≠

Multiple Primary Owners

⸻

CP-02 — Coordination Does Not Transfer Ownership

Unit yang berkoordinasi dengan unit lain tidak memperoleh ownership atas domain unit tersebut.

Coordination

≠

Ownership Transfer

⸻

CP-03 — Dependency Does Not Create Subordination

Dependency antar Platform atau unit organisasi tidak otomatis menciptakan reporting relationship.

Dependency

≠

Subordination

Prinsip ini secara eksplisit konsisten dengan distinction antara hierarchy dan dependency pada Platform Organization baseline.

Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

⸻

CP-04 — Interface Before Unbounded Interaction

Material cross-platform interaction harus memiliki interface boundary yang jelas.

B7 menjadi boundary reference.

⸻

CP-05 — Coordination Must Be Traceable

Material coordination harus dapat ditelusuri:

Coordination Need

↓

Participants

↓

Context

↓

Discussion / Analysis

↓

Decision or Agreement

↓

Action

↓

Outcome

⸻

CP-06 — Shared Outcome

Beberapa unit dapat bekerja untuk outcome yang sama tanpa kehilangan domain ownership.

⸻

CP-07 — Proportional Coordination

Coordination intensity harus proporsional terhadap:

• impact;

• dependency;

• complexity;

• risk;

• number of affected domains;

• decision significance.

Tidak semua pekerjaan memerlukan forum atau review formal.

⸻

CP-08 — Semantic Authority Preservation

Koordinasi Knowledge harus mempertahankan authority PD-04 atas canonical Knowledge semantics.

Consumer tidak boleh mengubah canonical semantic definition secara informal.

⸻

• responsibility saling bergantung;
