• review membutuhkan specialist lain;

• outcome membutuhkan collaboration.

Capability A

│ Dependency

47\. Coordination Anti-Patterns

AP-01 — Coordination Without Owner

Everyone coordinates

↓

No one owns

Tidak diperbolehkan.

⸻

AP-02 — Coordination as Hidden Authority

Forum tidak boleh memperoleh authority hanya karena menjadi forum.

⸻

AP-03 — Coordination as Reporting

Cross-team coordination tidak menjadi reporting hierarchy.

⸻

AP-04 — Coordination as Ownership

Participation tidak berarti ownership.

⸻

AP-05 — Coordination Without Trace

Material coordination tanpa record dapat menyebabkan loss of context.

⸻

AP-06 — Over-Coordination

Tidak semua issue membutuhkan multi-party governance.

⸻

AP-07 — Under-Coordination

Material cross-platform impact tidak boleh diperlakukan sebagai local-only issue.

⸻

AP-08 — Semantic Drift

Consumer tidak boleh mengubah canonical Knowledge semantics melalui local interpretation tanpa governance.

48\. Coordination Maturity

Coordination maturity dapat dinilai secara bounded:

Level 0 — Ad Hoc

No defined coordination mechanism

Level 1 — Identifiable

Participants and owner identifiable

Level 2 — Structured

Repeatable coordination pattern exists

Level 3 — Governed

Material coordination is traceable and reviewed

Level 4 — Integrated

Coordination is integrated with interface, dependency, and change management

Level 5 — Adaptive

Coordination improves through evidence and learning

Ini adalah reconstruction maturity model, bukan klaim bahwa baseline telah menetapkan maturity level PD-04.

58\. Canonical Freeze Statement

B8 — Knowledge & Intelligence Organizational Coordination Architecture establishes the coordination layer through which PD-04 organizes internal and cross-platform collaboration while preserving primary ownership, authority, reporting hierarchy, interface boundaries, semantic authority, and domain autonomy.

Coordination does not transfer ownership. Dependency does not create subordination. Participation does not create authority. Collaboration does not create multiple primary owners.

PD-04 remains the primary organizational owner of Knowledge & Intelligence and of its canonical Knowledge artifacts, including Knowledge Base, Knowledge Graph, and Ontology.

Cross-platform coordination with Architecture, Governance, Runtime, AI Engineering, Infrastructure, Security, Quality, and Developer Experience must preserve the ownership of each respective Platform domain.

The source establishes the underlying Platform Organization, artifact ownership, interface contracts, and cross-platform coordination principles. The exact B8 section is not established as a literal source section; therefore this section remains explicitly classified as Bounded Canonical Reconstruction.

B8 is FROZEN WITH SOURCE QUALIFICATION.

\- antar Team;

\- dengan Platform Division lain;

4\. Coordination Constitution

PD-04 coordination mengikuti prinsip:

Ownership

↓

Responsibility

5\. Coordination Principles

CP-01 — Ownership First

Setiap material responsibility harus memiliki primary owner.

Coordination tidak menciptakan ownership kedua.

Collaboration

≠

Multiple Primary Owners

⸻

CP-02 — Coordination Does Not Transfer Ownership

Unit yang berkoordinasi dengan unit lain tidak memperoleh ownership atas domain unit tersebut.

Coordination

≠

Ownership Transfer

⸻

CP-03 — Dependency Does Not Create Subordination

Dependency antar Platform atau unit organisasi tidak otomatis menciptakan reporting relationship.

Dependency

≠

Subordination

Prinsip ini secara eksplisit konsisten dengan distinction antara hierarchy dan dependency pada Platform Organization baseline.

Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

⸻

CP-04 — Interface Before Unbounded Interaction

Material cross-platform interaction harus memiliki interface boundary yang jelas.

B7 menjadi boundary reference.

⸻

CP-05 — Coordination Must Be Traceable

Material coordination harus dapat ditelusuri:

Coordination Need

↓

Participants

↓

Context

↓

Discussion / Analysis

↓

Decision or Agreement

↓

Action

↓

Outcome

⸻

CP-06 — Shared Outcome

Beberapa unit dapat bekerja untuk outcome yang sama tanpa kehilangan domain ownership.

⸻

CP-07 — Proportional Coordination

Coordination intensity harus proporsional terhadap:

• impact;

• dependency;

• complexity;

• risk;

• number of affected domains;

• decision significance.

Tidak semua pekerjaan memerlukan forum atau review formal.

⸻

CP-08 — Semantic Authority Preservation

Koordinasi Knowledge harus mempertahankan authority PD-04 atas canonical Knowledge semantics.

Consumer tidak boleh mengubah canonical semantic definition secara informal.

47\. Coordination Anti-Patterns

AP-01 — Coordination Without Owner

Everyone coordinates

↓

No one owns

Tidak diperbolehkan.

⸻

AP-02 — Coordination as Hidden Authority

Forum tidak boleh memperoleh authority hanya karena menjadi forum.

⸻

AP-03 — Coordination as Reporting

Cross-team coordination tidak menjadi reporting hierarchy.

⸻

AP-04 — Coordination as Ownership

Participation tidak berarti ownership.

⸻

AP-05 — Coordination Without Trace

Material coordination tanpa record dapat menyebabkan loss of context.

⸻

AP-06 — Over-Coordination

Tidak semua issue membutuhkan multi-party governance.

⸻

AP-07 — Under-Coordination

Material cross-platform impact tidak boleh diperlakukan sebagai local-only issue.

⸻

AP-08 — Semantic Drift

Consumer tidak boleh mengubah canonical Knowledge semantics melalui local interpretation tanpa governance.

48\. Coordination Maturity

Coordination maturity dapat dinilai secara bounded:

Level 0 — Ad Hoc

No defined coordination mechanism

Level 1 — Identifiable

Participants and owner identifiable

Level 2 — Structured

Repeatable coordination pattern exists

Level 3 — Governed

Material coordination is traceable and reviewed

Level 4 — Integrated

Coordination is integrated with interface, dependency, and change management

Level 5 — Adaptive

Coordination improves through evidence and learning

Ini adalah reconstruction maturity model, bukan klaim bahwa baseline telah menetapkan maturity level PD-04.

⸻

0\. quality findings dapat ditindaklanjuti;

0\. coordination tidak menghasilkan duplicate ownership;

0\. coordination intensity proporsional terhadap materiality;

0\. recurring coordination friction dapat menjadi improvement input.

58\. Canonical Freeze Statement

B8 — Knowledge & Intelligence Organizational Coordination Architecture establishes the coordination layer through which PD-04 organizes internal and cross-platform collaboration while preserving primary ownership, authority, reporting hierarchy, interface boundaries, semantic authority, and domain autonomy.

Coordination does not transfer ownership. Dependency does not create subordination. Participation does not create authority. Collaboration does not create multiple primary owners.

PD-04 remains the primary organizational owner of Knowledge & Intelligence and of its canonical Knowledge artifacts, including Knowledge Base, Knowledge Graph, and Ontology.

Cross-platform coordination with Architecture, Governance, Runtime, AI Engineering, Infrastructure, Security, Quality, and Developer Experience must preserve the ownership of each respective Platform domain.

The source establishes the underlying Platform Organization, artifact ownership, interface contracts, and cross-platform coordination principles. The exact B8 section is not established as a literal source section; therefore this section remains explicitly classified as Bounded Canonical Reconstruction.

B8 is FROZEN WITH SOURCE QUALIFICATION.

⸻

GP-06 — Dependency Does Not Create Subordination

Dependency terhadap Platform lain tidak menjadikan Platform tersebut subordinate kepada PD-04.

⸻

GP-07 — Evidence Before Material Conclusion

Material organizational governance conclusion harus memiliki evidence yang sesuai.

Claim

↓

Evidence

↓

Assessment

↓

Conclusion

Prinsip evidence-before-assertion konsisten dengan governance operating model yang tersedia dalam source.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

GP-08 — Traceability by Design

Material organizational governance activity harus dapat ditelusuri sepanjang lifecycle-nya.

⸻

GP-09 — Minimum Necessary Governance

Governance mechanism harus cukup untuk:

• menjaga ownership;

• menjaga accountability;

• menjaga authority;

• menjaga boundary;

• menjaga integrity.

Tidak boleh menciptakan bureaucracy tanpa kebutuhan.

⸻

GP-10 — Controlled Evolution

Perubahan organizational structure harus dikendalikan.

Organizational change tidak boleh terjadi hanya karena kebiasaan operasional.

⸻

8\. Organizational Governance Domains

B9 mengatur governance terhadap:

1\. Ownership Governance

2\. Accountability Governance

3\. Authority Governance

4\. Delegation Governance

5\. Decision Governance

6\. Review Governance

7\. Escalation Governance

8\. Change Governance

9\. Cross-Platform Governance

10\. Evidence & Traceability Governance

11\. Organizational Improvement Governance

Detail yang membutuhkan governance authority enterprise tetap berada pada applicable governance domain.

⸻

9\. Ownership Governance

Ownership governance memastikan:

• setiap material responsibility memiliki owner;

• owner dapat diidentifikasi;

• ownership tidak ambigu;

• ownership tidak berubah melalui coordination;

• ownership tidak berubah melalui dependency.

Canonical rule:

One Material Responsibility

↓

One Primary Owner

Collaboration dapat melibatkan banyak participant.

Collaboration tidak menghasilkan multiple primary owners.

⸻

10\. Knowledge Ownership Governance

PD-04 mempertahankan authority atas Knowledge domain.

Knowledge-related organizational governance harus melindungi ownership atas:

• Knowledge Model;

• Knowledge Repository;

• Semantic Layer;

• Context Knowledge;

• Knowledge Assets.

PD-04 tidak mengambil ownership atas Runtime atau Agent execution hanya karena Knowledge digunakan oleh domain tersebut.

⸻

11\. Intelligence Asset Governance

Intelligence Assets harus memiliki:

• owner;

• purpose;

• provenance;

• applicable lifecycle;

• consumer boundary;

• reviewability.

Governance tidak berarti setiap consumer memperoleh ownership.

⸻

12\. Accountability Governance

Setiap material organizational responsibility harus memiliki:

• responsible owner;

• accountable authority;

• applicable reviewer;

• expected outcome.

Model:

Responsibility

↓

Owner

↓

Accountability

↓

Outcome

Accountability harus tetap dapat ditelusuri ketika pekerjaan didelegasikan.

⸻

13\. Authority Governance

Authority pada PD-04 harus digunakan sesuai:

• platform mandate;

• organizational boundary;

• applicable architecture;

• applicable governance;

• delegation;

• decision scope.

Authority tidak boleh diperluas hanya berdasarkan praktik informal.

⸻

14\. Authority Boundary

PD-04 authority berada pada Knowledge & Intelligence domain.

PD-04 dapat mengambil keputusan yang berada dalam authority domain-nya.

PD-04 tidak dapat menggunakan Knowledge Authority untuk:

• mengubah Architecture Baseline secara sepihak;

• mengambil Runtime ownership;

• mengambil AI Engineering ownership;

• mengambil Security ownership;

• mengambil Quality ownership;

• mengambil enterprise governance authority.

⸻

15\. Delegation Governance

Delegation diperbolehkan apabila:

• delegator memiliki authority;

• delegated scope jelas;

• delegate dapat diidentifikasi;

• duration/status dapat ditentukan apabila applicable;

• accountability tetap jelas;

• delegation dapat ditelusuri.

Canonical rule:

Delegation

≠

Ownership Transfer

Delegation juga tidak boleh memperluas authority melebihi authority delegator.

⸻

16\. Decision Governance

Material organizational decisions harus memiliki:

• decision owner;

• decision context;

• relevant evidence;

• affected scope;

• applicable authority;

• outcome;

• traceability.

Canonical flow:

Issue / Need

↓

Context

↓

Analysis

↓

Decision

↓

Action

↓

Outcome

Decision governance tidak boleh membuat authority baru yang tidak berasal dari applicable authority model.

⸻

17\. Organizational Decision Classes

Material decisions dapat mencakup:

Structural Decision

Mengubah organizational structure.

Ownership Decision

Mengubah responsibility ownership.

Accountability Decision

Mengubah accountability arrangement.

Authority Decision

Mengubah applicable authority.

Delegation Decision

Memberikan delegated authority.

Interface Decision

Mengubah organizational interface.

Coordination Decision

Mengubah coordination mechanism.

Change Decision

Mengubah organizational configuration.

Setiap class harus mengikuti authority yang sesuai.

⸻

18\. Structural Decision Boundary

Perubahan:

• Sub Division;

• Capability;

• Team;

• Role Group;

• Reporting Hierarchy;

tidak boleh dilakukan hanya melalui operational coordination.

Structural change harus melalui applicable organizational architecture change mechanism.

⸻

19\. Review Governance

Material organizational governance harus dapat direview.

Review digunakan untuk memeriksa:

• ownership;

• accountability;

• authority;

• structure;

• boundary;

• dependency;

• effectiveness;

• evidence.

Review tidak otomatis berarti perubahan.

⸻

20\. Review Triggers

Review dapat dipicu oleh:

• material organizational change;

• recurring governance issue;

• ownership ambiguity;

• authority conflict;

• accountability gap;

• cross-platform boundary issue;

• structural inefficiency;

• major capability change;

• major Knowledge architecture change;

• strategic direction change;

• audit or assurance finding.

⸻

21\. Review Proportionality

Tidak semua review membutuhkan tingkat formalitas yang sama.

Materiality menentukan depth of treatment.

Low Materiality

↓

Light Review

Medium Materiality

↓

Structured Review

High Materiality

↓

Formal Review

⸻

22\. Escalation Governance

Escalation digunakan apabila:

• issue tidak dapat diselesaikan pada authority lokal;

• domain boundary tidak jelas;

• ownership conflict terjadi;

• authority conflict terjadi;

• cross-platform impact material;

• governance requirement tidak dapat dipenuhi;

• organizational decision melampaui local authority.

Canonical flow:

Issue

↓

Local Resolution

↓

Authority Check

↓

Escalation

↓

Applicable Authority

↓

Decision

↓

Record

⸻

PD-02 Architecture

PD-03 Governance & Compliance

PD-06 AI Engineering

PD-07 Infrastructure & Platform

PD-08 Security

PD-09 Quality & Evaluation

PD-10 Developer Experience

Namun:

Cross-Platform Governance

≠

Cross-Platform Ownership

Setiap Platform tetap memiliki domain ownership masing-masing.

PD-02 tetap memiliki Architecture ownership.

PD-04 tetap memiliki Knowledge ownership.

Namun PD-03 tidak memperoleh Knowledge ownership hanya karena governance requirement berlaku terhadap PD-04.

32\. Organizational Change Governance

Organizational change harus dibedakan dari operational change.

↓

B8 / Part D

Organizational Change

Mengubah structure, ownership,

authority, accountability,

atau reporting

↓

B9 / applicable governance

Tidak semua operational improvement merupakan organizational change.

33\. Organizational Change Categories

Material organizational change dapat mencakup:

• creation of organizational unit;

• removal of organizational unit;

• responsibility transfer;

• ownership change;

• reporting change;

• authority change;

• delegation model change;

• interface change;

• capability restructuring;

• team restructuring.

• affected responsibility;

• affected owner;

• affected capability;

• affected team;

• affected role group;

• affected reporting;

• affected interface;

• affected dependency;

• affected Knowledge assets;

• affected Platform;

• affected governance requirements.

Recommendation

Awaiting Authority

38\. Organizational Governance Metrics

B9 dapat menggunakan bounded metrics seperti:

• ownership ambiguity rate;

• unresolved accountability count;

• authority conflict count;

• unresolved escalation count;

• structural change lead time;

• review completion rate;

• governance finding recurrence;

• cross-platform governance issue count;

• decision traceability coverage.

Metrics ini adalah reconstructed measurement candidates, bukan klaim bahwa baseline telah menetapkan KPI tersebut.

⸻

39\. Governance Effectiveness

Organizational governance dianggap efektif apabila:

• ownership jelas;

• accountability jelas;

• authority digunakan sesuai boundary;

• decisions dapat ditelusuri;

• changes dikendalikan;

• conflicts dapat dieskalasikan;

• reviews dapat dilakukan;

• cross-platform boundaries tetap intact.

⸻

40\. Governance Maturity

Bounded maturity model:

Level 0 — Ad Hoc

Governance depends on individual practice.

Level 1 — Defined

Basic ownership and accountability are identifiable.

Level 2 — Structured

Governance mechanisms are repeatable.

Level 3 — Traceable

Material governance activity is evidence-based and traceable.

Level 4 — Integrated

Governance is integrated with structure, interfaces,

dependencies, and change.

Level 5 — Adaptive

Governance continuously improves from evidence and findings.

Model ini adalah reconstruction dan bukan source-established PD-04 maturity classification.

⸻

41\. Governance Anti-Patterns

AP-01 — Governance Without Owner

Tidak boleh ada governance activity tanpa owner.

⸻

AP-02 — Authority Without Boundary

Tidak boleh ada authority yang tidak memiliki scope.

⸻

AP-03 — Delegation Without Accountability

Delegation tidak boleh menghilangkan accountability.

⸻

AP-04 — Coordination as Authority

Coordination forum tidak otomatis memperoleh authority.

⸻

AP-05 — Dependency as Subordination

Dependency tidak boleh diubah menjadi reporting hierarchy.

⸻

AP-06 — Governance as Ownership Transfer

Governance requirement tidak memindahkan domain ownership.

⸻

AP-07 — Informal Structural Change

Structure tidak boleh berubah melalui kebiasaan operasional.

⸻

AP-08 — Evidence-Free Decision

Material governance conclusion tidak boleh hanya berdasarkan assertion.

⸻

AP-09 — Parallel Governance Hierarchy

B9 tidak boleh menciptakan hierarchy yang bertentangan dengan B6.

⸻

AP-10 — Governance Overreach

PD-04 governance mechanism tidak boleh berkembang menjadi enterprise Governance & Compliance function.

54\. Canonical Freeze Statement

B9 — Knowledge & Intelligence Organizational Governance Architecture establishes the governance layer for the organizational structure of PD-04 Knowledge & Intelligence.

B9 governs ownership, accountability, authority application, delegation, organizational decision, review, escalation, change, evidence, traceability, and improvement while preserving the organizational structure and reporting hierarchy established by B1–B8.

PD-04 retains primary ownership of the Knowledge & Intelligence domain and its organizational responsibilities. Governance requirements originating from PD-03 do not transfer Knowledge ownership to PD-03. Architecture, Runtime, AI Engineering, Infrastructure, Security, Quality, and Developer Experience remain owned by their respective Platform Divisions.

Coordination does not create authority. Dependency does not create subordination. Delegation does not automatically transfer ownership. Governance does not create a parallel organizational hierarchy.

The available source establishes the underlying Platform identity, Knowledge Authority, organizational model, ownership principles, and governance principles, but does not establish the exact B9 section as a literal canonical source section. B9 is therefore explicitly classified as Bounded Canonical Reconstruction.

B9 is FROZEN WITH SOURCE QUALIFICATION.

56\. Final Architectural Decision

B9 = PASS — APPROVED WITH SOURCE QUALIFICATION.

The canonical organizational governance distinction is:

Ownership

≠

Authority

≠

Accountability

≠

Delegation

≠

Reporting

≠

Interface

≠

Coordination

≠

Governance

The governance model is:

Organizational Responsibility

↓

Owner

↓

Authority

↓

Accountability

↓

Governance

↓

Evidence

↓

Review

↓

Decision / Change

↓

Validation

↓

Improvement

Therefore:

B9 provides the organizational governance layer required to keep PD-04 Knowledge & Intelligence coherent, accountable, bounded, and evolvable while preserving Knowledge Authority, domain ownership, reporting integrity, and cross-platform autonomy.

\*\*Catatan penting:\*\* Saya sengaja \*\*tidak mengklaim B9 sebagai source literal\*\*. Source yang ditemukan paling kuat justru memberi \*governance principles\* dan organizational boundary, sementara identity B9 spesifik belum ditemukan. Ini konsisten dengan pola yang kita gunakan: \*\*source fidelity → bounded reconstruction → freeze → construction\*\*.

7\. Governance Principles

GP-01 — Domain Ownership First

Governance harus dimulai dari domain ownership.

Domain

↓

Owner

↓

Responsibility

↓

Governance

Governance tidak menciptakan ownership baru.

⸻

GP-02 — Authority Does Not Equal Ownership

Authority yang digunakan dalam governance tidak otomatis mengubah organizational ownership.

Authority

≠

Ownership

⸻

GP-03 — Accountability Must Remain Explicit

Setiap material organizational responsibility harus memiliki accountability context.

Delegation tidak menghapus accountability.

⸻

GP-04 — Governance Does Not Create Parallel Hierarchy

B9 tidak membuat hierarchy yang berjalan paralel terhadap B6.

B6

Reporting Hierarchy

-

B9

Governance

Governance berjalan melalui hierarchy yang telah ditetapkan.

⸻

GP-05 — Coordination Does Not Create Authority

B8 coordination relationship tidak otomatis menghasilkan decision authority.

Coordination

≠

Authority

⸻

GP-06 — Dependency Does Not Create Subordination

Dependency terhadap Platform lain tidak menjadikan Platform tersebut subordinate kepada PD-04.

⸻

GP-07 — Evidence Before Material Conclusion

Material organizational governance conclusion harus memiliki evidence yang sesuai.

Claim

↓

Evidence

↓

Assessment

↓

Conclusion

Prinsip evidence-before-assertion konsisten dengan governance operating model yang tersedia dalam source.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

GP-08 — Traceability by Design

Material organizational governance activity harus dapat ditelusuri sepanjang lifecycle-nya.

⸻

GP-09 — Minimum Necessary Governance

Governance mechanism harus cukup untuk:

• menjaga ownership;

• menjaga accountability;

• menjaga authority;

• menjaga boundary;

• menjaga integrity.

Tidak boleh menciptakan bureaucracy tanpa kebutuhan.

⸻

GP-10 — Controlled Evolution

Perubahan organizational structure harus dikendalikan.

Organizational change tidak boleh terjadi hanya karena kebiasaan operasional.

⸻

8\. Organizational Governance Domains

B9 mengatur governance terhadap:

1\. Ownership Governance

2\. Accountability Governance

3\. Authority Governance

Detail yang membutuhkan governance authority enterprise tetap berada pada applicable governance domain.

⸻

9\. Ownership Governance

Ownership governance memastikan:

• setiap material responsibility memiliki owner;

• owner dapat diidentifikasi;

• ownership tidak ambigu;

• ownership tidak berubah melalui coordination;

• ownership tidak berubah melalui dependency.

Canonical rule:

One Material Responsibility

↓

One Primary Owner

Collaboration dapat melibatkan banyak participant.

Collaboration tidak menghasilkan multiple primary owners.

⸻

10\. Knowledge Ownership Governance

PD-04 mempertahankan authority atas Knowledge domain.

Knowledge-related organizational governance harus melindungi ownership atas:

• Knowledge Model;

• Knowledge Repository;

• Semantic Layer;

• Context Knowledge;

• Knowledge Assets.

PD-04 tidak mengambil ownership atas Runtime atau Agent execution hanya karena Knowledge digunakan oleh domain tersebut.

⸻

11\. Intelligence Asset Governance

Intelligence Assets harus memiliki:

• owner;

• purpose;

• provenance;

• applicable lifecycle;

• consumer boundary;

• reviewability.

Governance tidak berarti setiap consumer memperoleh ownership.

⸻

12\. Accountability Governance

Setiap material organizational responsibility harus memiliki:

• responsible owner;

• accountable authority;

• applicable reviewer;

• expected outcome.

Model:

Responsibility

↓

Owner

↓

Accountability

↓

Outcome

Accountability harus tetap dapat ditelusuri ketika pekerjaan didelegasikan.

⸻

13\. Authority Governance

Authority pada PD-04 harus digunakan sesuai:

• platform mandate;

• organizational boundary;

• applicable architecture;

• applicable governance;

• delegation;

• decision scope.

Authority tidak boleh diperluas hanya berdasarkan praktik informal.

⸻

14\. Authority Boundary

PD-04 authority berada pada Knowledge & Intelligence domain.

PD-04 dapat mengambil keputusan yang berada dalam authority domain-nya.

PD-04 tidak dapat menggunakan Knowledge Authority untuk:

• mengubah Architecture Baseline secara sepihak;

• mengambil Runtime ownership;

• mengambil AI Engineering ownership;

• mengambil Security ownership;

• mengambil Quality ownership;

• mengambil enterprise governance authority.

⸻

15\. Delegation Governance

Delegation diperbolehkan apabila:

• delegator memiliki authority;

• delegated scope jelas;

• delegate dapat diidentifikasi;

• duration/status dapat ditentukan apabila applicable;

• accountability tetap jelas;

• delegation dapat ditelusuri.

Canonical rule:

Delegation

≠

Ownership Transfer

Delegation juga tidak boleh memperluas authority melebihi authority delegator.

⸻

16\. Decision Governance

Material organizational decisions harus memiliki:

• decision owner;

• decision context;

• relevant evidence;

• affected scope;

• applicable authority;

• outcome;

• traceability.

Canonical flow:

Issue / Need

↓

Context

↓

Analysis

↓

Decision

↓

Action

↓

Outcome

Decision governance tidak boleh membuat authority baru yang tidak berasal dari applicable authority model.

⸻

17\. Organizational Decision Classes

Material decisions dapat mencakup:

Structural Decision

Mengubah organizational structure.

Ownership Decision

Mengubah responsibility ownership.

Accountability Decision

Mengubah accountability arrangement.

Authority Decision

Mengubah applicable authority.

Delegation Decision

Memberikan delegated authority.

Interface Decision

Mengubah organizational interface.

Coordination Decision

Mengubah coordination mechanism.

Change Decision

Mengubah organizational configuration.

Setiap class harus mengikuti authority yang sesuai.

⸻

18\. Structural Decision Boundary

Perubahan:

• Sub Division;

• Capability;

• Team;

• Role Group;

• Reporting Hierarchy;

tidak boleh dilakukan hanya melalui operational coordination.

Structural change harus melalui applicable organizational architecture change mechanism.

⸻

19\. Review Governance

Material organizational governance harus dapat direview.

Review digunakan untuk memeriksa:

• ownership;

• accountability;

• authority;

• structure;

• boundary;

• dependency;

• effectiveness;

• evidence.

Review tidak otomatis berarti perubahan.

⸻

20\. Review Triggers

Review dapat dipicu oleh:

• material organizational change;

• recurring governance issue;

• ownership ambiguity;

• authority conflict;

• accountability gap;

• cross-platform boundary issue;

• structural inefficiency;

• major capability change;

• major Knowledge architecture change;

• strategic direction change;

• audit or assurance finding.

⸻

21\. Review Proportionality

Tidak semua review membutuhkan tingkat formalitas yang sama.

Materiality menentukan depth of treatment.

Low Materiality

↓

Light Review

Medium Materiality

↓

Structured Review

High Materiality

↓

Formal Review

⸻

22\. Escalation Governance

Escalation digunakan apabila:

• issue tidak dapat diselesaikan pada authority lokal;

• domain boundary tidak jelas;

• ownership conflict terjadi;

• authority conflict terjadi;

• cross-platform impact material;

• governance requirement tidak dapat dipenuhi;

• organizational decision melampaui local authority.

Canonical flow:

Issue

↓

Local Resolution

↓

Authority Check

↓

Escalation

↓

Applicable Authority

↓

Decision

↓

Record

39\. Governance Effectiveness

• ownership jelas;

• conflicts dapat dieskalasikan;

40\. Governance Maturity

Bounded maturity model:

Level 0 — Ad Hoc

Governance depends on individual practice.

Level 1 — Defined

Basic ownership and accountability are identifiable.

Level 2 — Structured

Governance mechanisms are repeatable.

Level 3 — Traceable

Material governance activity is evidence-based and traceable.

Level 4 — Integrated

Governance is integrated with structure, interfaces,

dependencies, and change.

Level 5 — Adaptive

Governance continuously improves from evidence and findings.

Model ini adalah reconstruction dan bukan source-established PD-04 maturity classification.

⸻

41\. Governance Anti-Patterns

AP-01 — Governance Without Owner

Tidak boleh ada governance activity tanpa owner.

⸻

AP-02 — Authority Without Boundary

Tidak boleh ada authority yang tidak memiliki scope.

⸻

AP-03 — Delegation Without Accountability

Delegation tidak boleh menghilangkan accountability.

⸻

AP-04 — Coordination as Authority

Coordination forum tidak otomatis memperoleh authority.

⸻

AP-05 — Dependency as Subordination

Dependency tidak boleh diubah menjadi reporting hierarchy.

⸻

AP-06 — Governance as Ownership Transfer

Governance requirement tidak memindahkan domain ownership.

⸻

AP-07 — Informal Structural Change

Structure tidak boleh berubah melalui kebiasaan operasional.

⸻

AP-08 — Evidence-Free Decision

Material governance conclusion tidak boleh hanya berdasarkan assertion.

⸻

AP-09 — Parallel Governance Hierarchy

B9 tidak boleh menciptakan hierarchy yang bertentangan dengan B6.

⸻

AP-10 — Governance Overreach

PD-04 governance mechanism tidak boleh berkembang menjadi enterprise Governance & Compliance function.

54\. Canonical Freeze Statement

B9 — Knowledge & Intelligence Organizational Governance Architecture establishes the governance layer for the organizational structure of PD-04 Knowledge & Intelligence.

B9 governs ownership, accountability, authority application, delegation, organizational decision, review, escalation, change, evidence, traceability, and improvement while preserving the organizational structure and reporting hierarchy established by B1–B8.

PD-04 retains primary ownership of the Knowledge & Intelligence domain and its organizational responsibilities. Governance requirements originating from PD-03 do not transfer Knowledge ownership to PD-03. Architecture, Runtime, AI Engineering, Infrastructure, Security, Quality, and Developer Experience remain owned by their respective Platform Divisions.

Coordination does not create authority. Dependency does not create subordination. Delegation does not automatically transfer ownership. Governance does not create a parallel organizational hierarchy.

The available source establishes the underlying Platform identity, Knowledge Authority, organizational model, ownership principles, and governance principles, but does not establish the exact B9 section as a literal canonical source section. B9 is therefore explicitly classified as Bounded Canonical Reconstruction.

B9 is FROZEN WITH SOURCE QUALIFICATION.

Architecture\*\*.

4\. Part B Success Boundary

Part B berhasil apabila organisasi PD-04:

0\. memiliki ownership yang jelas;

0\. memiliki accountability yang jelas;

0\. memiliki organizational structure yang coherent;

0\. memiliki capability coverage yang memadai;

0\. memiliki Team dan Role Group yang dapat menjalankan responsibility;

0\. memiliki reporting hierarchy yang tidak ambigu;

0\. memiliki organizational interface yang jelas;

0\. dapat berkoordinasi dengan Platform lain;

0\. dapat menjalankan governance;

0\. dapat mempertahankan domain boundary;

0\. dapat berkembang tanpa structural drift;

0\. mampu menghasilkan outcome organisasi yang sesuai mandate.

6\. Primary Success Principle

PD-04 Organizational Success exists when the Knowledge & Intelligence organization can sustainably execute its domain mandate while preserving ownership, accountability, authority, organizational integrity, and cross-platform boundaries.

⸻

7\. Success Dimension Model

B10 menggunakan tujuh success dimensions:

1\. Organizational Effectiveness

2\. Ownership & Accountability Integrity

3\. Capability Coverage

4\. Organizational Coordination

5\. Governance Integrity

6\. Cross-Platform Alignment

7\. Organizational Maturity & Sustainability

Model ini adalah bounded reconstruction untuk PD-04.

⸻

8\. SD-01 — Organizational Effectiveness

Objective

Memastikan struktur organisasi PD-04 mampu menjalankan responsibility Knowledge & Intelligence secara efektif.

Success Conditions

PD-04 harus:

• mampu menjalankan mandate;

• memiliki responsibility coverage;

• memiliki clear organizational ownership;

• menghindari material organizational gaps;

• menghindari unnecessary structural duplication;

• menghasilkan expected organizational outcomes.

Evidence Candidates

• Organizational Responsibility Map;

• Capability Map;

• Team Responsibility Map;

• Organizational Review Record;

• Success Assessment Record.

⸻

9\. SD-02 — Ownership & Accountability Integrity

Objective

Memastikan setiap material organizational responsibility memiliki owner dan accountability yang jelas.

Success Conditions

• primary ownership dapat diidentifikasi;

• accountability tidak ambigu;

• delegation tidak menghapus accountability;

• collaboration tidak menciptakan conflicting ownership;

• cross-platform dependency tidak memindahkan ownership;

• ownership tetap sesuai Platform boundary.

Source secara eksplisit mempertahankan prinsip bahwa setiap Platform memiliki own domain success criteria dan ownership masing-masing.

Cabang Cabang Cabang Cabang Cabang Cabang Cabang Cabang Cabang .txt

⸻

10\. Ownership Integrity Gate

PD-04 tidak dianggap organizationally successful apabila:

Material Responsibility

↓

No Owner

atau:

One Responsibility

↓

Conflicting Primary Owners

kecuali boundary tersebut secara eksplisit dibagi dan governed.

Canonical rule:

One Material Responsibility

↓

One Primary Owner

Supporting participants dapat lebih dari satu.

⸻

11\. SD-03 — Capability Coverage

Objective

Memastikan organizational structure memiliki capability coverage yang cukup untuk menjalankan Knowledge & Intelligence mandate.

Success Conditions

• material responsibility memiliki capability home;

• capability memiliki organizational owner;

• capability dapat diterjemahkan ke Team;

• Team dapat menjalankan required responsibility;

• tidak terdapat material capability gap yang tidak ditangani;

• capability overlap dapat dijelaskan.

B10 tidak menetapkan nama capability baru.

Capability definition tetap berasal dari B3.

⸻

Responsibility

↓

Capability

↓

↓

↓

Accountability

Success = NOT YET ESTABLISHED

atau:

Success = INSUFFICIENT EVIDENCE

tergantung kualitas evidence.

• coordination mechanism digunakan;

• dependency dapat dikelola;

• cross-team coordination dapat dilakukan;

• cross-platform coordination dapat dilakukan;

• coordination issue dapat dieskalasikan;

• coordination tidak berubah menjadi hidden hierarchy.

Relationship:

↓

B8

Coordination

↓

B8 tetap menjadi source of coordination mechanism.

14\. SD-05 — Governance Integrity

Objective

Memastikan organisasi PD-04 dapat beroperasi dalam governance boundary yang telah ditetapkan B9.

Success Conditions

• authority digunakan sesuai boundary;

• accountability dapat ditelusuri;

• delegation dapat dikendalikan;

• material decision memiliki applicable authority;

• review dapat dilakukan;

• escalation tersedia;

• organizational change dikendalikan;

• governance evidence tersedia.

B10 tidak menggantikan B9.

15\. SD-06 — Cross-Platform Alignment

Objective

Memastikan PD-04 dapat bekerja dengan Platform lain tanpa kehilangan Knowledge & Intelligence ownership.

Primary relationships dapat mencakup:

PD-02 → Architecture

PD-03 → Governance & Compliance

PD-05 → Runtime & Execution

PD-06 → AI Engineering

PD-07 → Infrastructure & Platform

PD-08 → Security

PD-09 → Quality & Evaluation

PD-04 tetap menjadi domain owner Knowledge & Intelligence.

16\. Cross-Platform Success Conditions

PD-04 dianggap aligned apabila:

• dependency dapat diidentifikasi;

• interface dapat digunakan;

• shared responsibility memiliki boundary;

• conflict dapat diselesaikan;

• governance requirement dapat dipenuhi;

• ownership tidak berpindah secara implisit;

• dependency tidak berubah menjadi subordination.

Interface

≠

Hierarchy

Dependency

≠

Ownership

Coordination

≠

Authority

⸻

17\. SD-07 — Organizational Maturity & Sustainability

Objective

Memastikan organisasi PD-04 tidak hanya dapat berjalan sekarang, tetapi dapat dipertahankan dan dikembangkan.

Success Conditions

• structure cukup stabil;

• capability dapat berkembang;

• Team dapat direstrukturisasi tanpa merusak domain ownership;

• Role Group dapat berkembang;

• governance dapat beradaptasi;

• organizational learning berjalan;

• recurring organizational problems menurun;

• material success tidak bergantung pada single individual.

⸻

18\. Organizational Maturity Model

Bounded maturity model:

Level 0 — Ad Hoc

Organizational responsibility dan accountability masih bergantung pada informal practice.

Level 1 — Defined

Ownership, responsibility, dan basic structure telah ditentukan.

Level 2 — Structured

Capability, Team, Role Group, reporting, interface, dan coordination mulai berjalan sebagai integrated structure.

Level 3 — Governed

Organization architecture berjalan dengan authority, accountability, review, escalation, dan controlled change.

Level 4 — Integrated

Organization, capability, coordination, governance, dan cross-platform relationship bekerja sebagai satu sistem.

Level 5 — Adaptive

Organisasi mampu belajar, berkembang, dan beradaptasi tanpa kehilangan domain identity atau constitutional boundary.

Level ini adalah \*\*reconstructed maturity model\*\*, bukan klaim bahwa

baseline menetapkan nomenklatur tersebut.

\---

\# 19. Integrated Organizational Success Model

PD-04 organizational success harus memenuhi:

\`\`\`text

Organizational Effectiveness

AND

Ownership & Accountability Integrity

AND

Capability Coverage

AND

Coordination Effectiveness

AND

Governance Integrity

AND

Cross-Platform Alignment

AND

Organizational Maturity

AND merupakan architectural gate.

Success tidak boleh ditentukan dengan simple average.

⸻

20\. Critical Success Conditions

Critical failure pada salah satu kondisi berikut dapat memblokir overall success claim:

0\. Knowledge ownership tidak jelas;

0\. material responsibility tidak memiliki owner;

0\. accountability material tidak dapat ditentukan;

0\. organizational boundary rusak;

0\. authority conflict tidak dapat diselesaikan;

0\. material capability gap tidak memiliki response;

0\. cross-platform dependency menyebabkan ownership takeover;

0\. reporting hierarchy memiliki contradiction material;

0\. governance architecture tidak dapat diterapkan;

0\. organizational structure tidak dapat menjalankan mandate.

⸻

21\. Success Status Model

B10 menggunakan status:

SUCCESSFUL

CONDITIONALLY SUCCESSFUL

NOT YET SUCCESSFUL

INSUFFICIENT EVIDENCE

BLOCKED

SUCCESSFUL

Seluruh critical conditions terpenuhi dan evidence memadai.

CONDITIONALLY SUCCESSFUL

Mayoritas conditions terpenuhi tetapi terdapat bounded non-critical gaps dengan remediation yang telah disetujui.

NOT YET SUCCESSFUL

Material success conditions belum terpenuhi.

INSUFFICIENT EVIDENCE

Evidence tidak cukup untuk membuat trustworthy conclusion.

BLOCKED

Success assessment tidak dapat diselesaikan karena material authority, dependency, governance, atau organizational condition belum resolved.

⸻

22\. Evidence Before Success Claim

Success claim harus mengikuti:

Success Criterion

↓

Evidence

↓

Assessment

↓

Conclusion

Tidak boleh:

Desired Outcome

↓

Success Claim

Evidence harus relevan terhadap criterion yang dinilai.

26\. Organizational Gap Model

Organizational gap harus dibedakan menjadi:

Ownership Gap

Responsibility tidak memiliki owner yang jelas.

Capability Gap

Required capability belum tersedia secara memadai.

Workforce / Role Gap

Role Group atau Team belum mampu memenuhi responsibility.

Governance Gap

Authority, accountability, review, atau escalation belum memadai.

Coordination Gap

Interface atau coordination mechanism tidak efektif.

Boundary Gap

Domain ownership atau organizational boundary ambigu.

Maturity Gap

Organisasi belum mencapai maturity yang diperlukan.

Observed Condition

↓

Evidence

↓

↓

Gap Classification

↓

Materiality

↓

Authorized Response

Gap tidak boleh langsung diterjemahkan menjadi structural redesign.

28\. Success vs Activity

B10 tidak menganggap:

• meeting selesai;

• dokumen dibuat;

• Team terbentuk;

• role ditetapkan;

• review dilakukan;

sebagai bukti success dengan sendirinya.

Activity adalah evidence candidate.

Success membutuhkan outcome.

Struktur organisasi yang lengkap tidak otomatis berarti organisasi berhasil.

Structure

≠

Effectiveness

Struktur harus menghasilkan kemampuan menjalankan mandate.

30\. Success vs Compliance

Compliance terhadap governance requirement bukan keseluruhan organizational success.

Compliance

≠

Organizational Success

31\. Success vs Capability Existence

Capability yang tercatat dalam organizational model tidak otomatis membuktikan capability effectiveness.

≠

Jumlah coordination event tidak menentukan coordination effectiveness.

Assessment harus melihat:

• dependency resolution;

• ownership preservation;

• outcome.

33\. Success vs Maturity

Maturity

≠

Effectiveness

34\. Success vs Individual Performance

PD-04 organizational success tidak boleh bergantung pada penilaian satu individu.

Organizational architecture harus tetap valid apabila workforce implementation berubah.

35\. Sustainability Principle

Success hanya sustainable apabila:

• ownership tetap jelas;

• capability dapat dipertahankan;

• organizational knowledge tidak bergantung pada single individual;

• governance tetap berjalan;

• organizational interfaces tetap valid;

• material dependency tetap manageable.

⸻

36\. Regression Principle

Historical success tidak memberikan permanent immunity.

Success harus dapat direassess apabila terjadi:

• material organizational change;

• major capability change;

• ownership change;

• authority change;

• major cross-platform dependency change;

• significant governance change;

• material organizational failure;

• maturity regression.

⸻

OR

Material Organizational Failure

54\. Organizational Success Anti-Patterns

AP-01 — Structure Equals Success

Menganggap organisasi berhasil hanya karena struktur selesai.

⸻

AP-02 — Activity Equals Success

Menganggap aktivitas selesai sebagai proof of outcome.

⸻

AP-03 — Governance Equals Success

Menganggap compliance sebagai keseluruhan organizational success.

⸻

AP-04 — Capability Exists Equals Capability Works

Menganggap capability yang terdaftar otomatis efektif.

⸻

AP-05 — Coordination Count

Menggunakan jumlah meeting atau coordination event sebagai proxy effectiveness.

⸻

AP-06 — Ownership Drift

Mengubah dependency menjadi ownership.

⸻

AP-07 — Authority Inflation

Menggunakan success assessment untuk memperoleh authority baru.

⸻

AP-08 — Maturity Inflation

Menyatakan maturity tinggi tanpa evidence.

⸻

AP-09 — Evidence Suppression

Mengabaikan evidence yang bertentangan dengan success claim.

⸻

AP-10 — Permanent Success

Menganggap historical success tidak perlu reassessment.

0\. organizational success dapat dinilai;

0\. evidence untuk success assessment tersedia atau status INSUFFICIENT EVIDENCE dinyatakan secara eksplisit.

63\. Canonical Freeze Statement

B10 — Knowledge & Intelligence Organizational Success establishes the terminal acceptance model for Part B — Organization Architecture of PD-04.

PD-04 organizational success requires more than structural completeness. It requires organizational effectiveness, ownership and accountability integrity, capability coverage, coordination effectiveness, governance integrity, cross-platform alignment, and organizational maturity.

PD-04 retains ownership of the Knowledge & Intelligence domain. Cross-platform dependency, governance relationship, architectural interface, or collaboration does not transfer that ownership to another Platform.

Organizational success must be supported by evidence and must remain subject to reassessment after material organizational, capability, governance, ownership, or cross-platform change.

Success is an accepted state, not permanent immunity from reassessment.

The exact B10 section is not established as literal source in the available baseline. This section is therefore explicitly classified as Bounded Canonical Reconstruction.

B10 is FROZEN WITH SOURCE QUALIFICATION.

dipertahankan.

\> bounded, coordinated, governable, dan sustainable?\*\*

Architecture\*\*.

B10 — Knowledge & Intelligence Organizational Success

NOT ESTABLISHED AS LITERAL SOURCE.

• governance success harus mempertahankan clarity, accountability, collaboration, compliance, dan continuous improvement;

4\. Part B Success Boundary

Part B berhasil apabila organisasi PD-04:

0\. memiliki ownership yang jelas;

0\. memiliki accountability yang jelas;

0\. memiliki organizational structure yang coherent;

0\. memiliki capability coverage yang memadai;

0\. memiliki Team dan Role Group yang dapat menjalankan responsibility;

0\. memiliki reporting hierarchy yang tidak ambigu;

0\. memiliki organizational interface yang jelas;

0\. dapat berkoordinasi dengan Platform lain;

0\. dapat menjalankan governance;

0\. dapat mempertahankan domain boundary;

0\. dapat berkembang tanpa structural drift;

0\. mampu menghasilkan outcome organisasi yang sesuai mandate.

⸻

5\. Organizational Success Constitution

Organizational success PD-04 harus dipahami sebagai:

Mandate

↓

Responsibility

↓

Organization

↓

Capability

↓

Team

↓

Role Group

↓

Authority / Accountability

↓

Coordination

↓

Governance

↓

Outcome

↓

Success tidak ditentukan hanya dari keberadaan struktur.

⸻

6\. Primary Success Principle

PD-04 Organizational Success exists when the Knowledge & Intelligence organization can sustainably execute its domain mandate while preserving ownership, accountability, authority, organizational integrity, and cross-platform boundaries.

⸻

7\. Success Dimension Model

B10 menggunakan tujuh success dimensions:

1\. Organizational Effectiveness

2\. Ownership & Accountability Integrity

3\. Capability Coverage

4\. Organizational Coordination

5\. Governance Integrity

6\. Cross-Platform Alignment

7\. Organizational Maturity & Sustainability

Model ini adalah bounded reconstruction untuk PD-04.

⸻

8\. SD-01 — Organizational Effectiveness

Objective

Memastikan struktur organisasi PD-04 mampu menjalankan responsibility Knowledge & Intelligence secara efektif.

Success Conditions

PD-04 harus:

• mampu menjalankan mandate;

• memiliki responsibility coverage;

• memiliki clear organizational ownership;

• menghindari material organizational gaps;

• menghindari unnecessary structural duplication;

• menghasilkan expected organizational outcomes.

Evidence Candidates

• Organizational Responsibility Map;

• Capability Map;

• Team Responsibility Map;

• Organizational Review Record;

• Success Assessment Record.

⸻

9\. SD-02 — Ownership & Accountability Integrity

Objective

Memastikan setiap material organizational responsibility memiliki owner dan accountability yang jelas.

Success Conditions

• primary ownership dapat diidentifikasi;

• accountability tidak ambigu;

• delegation tidak menghapus accountability;

• collaboration tidak menciptakan conflicting ownership;

• cross-platform dependency tidak memindahkan ownership;

• ownership tetap sesuai Platform boundary.

Source secara eksplisit mempertahankan prinsip bahwa setiap Platform memiliki own domain success criteria dan ownership masing-masing.

Cabang Cabang Cabang Cabang Cabang Cabang Cabang Cabang Cabang .txt

⸻

10\. Ownership Integrity Gate

PD-04 tidak dianggap organizationally successful apabila:

Material Responsibility

↓

No Owner

atau:

One Responsibility

↓

Conflicting Primary Owners

kecuali boundary tersebut secara eksplisit dibagi dan governed.

Canonical rule:

One Material Responsibility

↓

One Primary Owner

Supporting participants dapat lebih dari satu.

⸻

11\. SD-03 — Capability Coverage

Objective

Memastikan organizational structure memiliki capability coverage yang cukup untuk menjalankan Knowledge & Intelligence mandate.

Success Conditions

• material responsibility memiliki capability home;

• capability memiliki organizational owner;

• capability dapat diterjemahkan ke Team;

• Team dapat menjalankan required responsibility;

• tidak terdapat material capability gap yang tidak ditangani;

• capability overlap dapat dijelaskan.

B10 tidak menetapkan nama capability baru.

Capability definition tetap berasal dari B3.

⸻

12\. Capability Coverage Test

Untuk setiap material responsibility:

Responsibility

↓

Capability

↓

Team

↓

Role Group

↓

Accountability

Jika chain terputus secara material:

Success = NOT YET ESTABLISHED

atau:

Success = INSUFFICIENT EVIDENCE

tergantung kualitas evidence.

⸻

13\. SD-04 — Organizational Coordination

Objective

Memastikan organisasi PD-04 dapat bekerja secara coordinated tanpa mengaburkan ownership.

Success Conditions

• organizational interfaces berfungsi;

• coordination mechanism digunakan;

• dependency dapat dikelola;

• cross-team coordination dapat dilakukan;

• cross-platform coordination dapat dilakukan;

• coordination issue dapat dieskalasikan;

• coordination tidak berubah menjadi hidden hierarchy.

Relationship:

B7

Interface

↓

B8

Coordination

↓

B10

Coordination Effectiveness

B10 menilai outcome coordination.

B8 tetap menjadi source of coordination mechanism.

⸻

14\. SD-05 — Governance Integrity

Objective

Memastikan organisasi PD-04 dapat beroperasi dalam governance boundary yang telah ditetapkan B9.

Success Conditions

• authority digunakan sesuai boundary;

• accountability dapat ditelusuri;

• delegation dapat dikendalikan;

• material decision memiliki applicable authority;

• review dapat dilakukan;

• escalation tersedia;

• organizational change dikendalikan;

• governance evidence tersedia.

B10 tidak menggantikan B9.

B9 menentukan governance mechanism.

B10 menentukan apakah governance tersebut mendukung organizational success.

⸻

15\. SD-06 — Cross-Platform Alignment

Objective

Memastikan PD-04 dapat bekerja dengan Platform lain tanpa kehilangan Knowledge & Intelligence ownership.

Primary relationships dapat mencakup:

PD-02 → Architecture

PD-03 → Governance & Compliance

PD-05 → Runtime & Execution

PD-06 → AI Engineering

PD-07 → Infrastructure & Platform

PD-08 → Security

PD-09 → Quality & Evaluation

PD-10 → Developer Experience

PD-04 tetap menjadi domain owner Knowledge & Intelligence.

⸻

16\. Cross-Platform Success Conditions

PD-04 dianggap aligned apabila:

• dependency dapat diidentifikasi;

• interface dapat digunakan;

• shared responsibility memiliki boundary;

• conflict dapat diselesaikan;

• governance requirement dapat dipenuhi;

• ownership tidak berpindah secara implisit;

• dependency tidak berubah menjadi subordination.

Canonical distinction:

Interface

≠

Hierarchy

Dependency

≠

Ownership

Coordination

≠

Authority

⸻

17\. SD-07 — Organizational Maturity & Sustainability

Objective

Memastikan organisasi PD-04 tidak hanya dapat berjalan sekarang, tetapi dapat dipertahankan dan dikembangkan.

Success Conditions

• structure cukup stabil;

• capability dapat berkembang;

• Team dapat direstrukturisasi tanpa merusak domain ownership;

• Role Group dapat berkembang;

• governance dapat beradaptasi;

• organizational learning berjalan;

• recurring organizational problems menurun;

• material success tidak bergantung pada single individual.

⸻

18\. Organizational Maturity Model

Bounded maturity model:

Level 0 — Ad Hoc

Organizational responsibility dan accountability masih bergantung pada informal practice.

Level 1 — Defined

Ownership, responsibility, dan basic structure telah ditentukan.

Level 2 — Structured

Capability, Team, Role Group, reporting, interface, dan coordination mulai berjalan sebagai integrated structure.

Level 3 — Governed

Organization architecture berjalan dengan authority, accountability, review, escalation, dan controlled change.

Level 4 — Integrated

Organization, capability, coordination, governance, dan cross-platform relationship bekerja sebagai satu sistem.

Level 5 — Adaptive

Organisasi mampu belajar, berkembang, dan beradaptasi tanpa kehilangan domain identity atau constitutional boundary.

Level ini adalah \*\*reconstructed maturity model\*\*, bukan klaim bahwa

baseline menetapkan nomenklatur tersebut.

\# 19. Integrated Organizational Success Model

PD-04 organizational success harus memenuhi:

\`\`\`text

Organizational Effectiveness

AND

Ownership & Accountability Integrity

AND

Capability Coverage

AND

Coordination Effectiveness

AND

Governance Integrity

AND

Cross-Platform Alignment

AND

Organizational Maturity

AND merupakan architectural gate.

Success tidak boleh ditentukan dengan simple average.

⸻

20\. Critical Success Conditions

Critical failure pada salah satu kondisi berikut dapat memblokir overall success claim:

0\. Knowledge ownership tidak jelas;

0\. material responsibility tidak memiliki owner;

0\. accountability material tidak dapat ditentukan;

0\. organizational boundary rusak;

0\. authority conflict tidak dapat diselesaikan;

0\. material capability gap tidak memiliki response;

0\. cross-platform dependency menyebabkan ownership takeover;

0\. reporting hierarchy memiliki contradiction material;

0\. governance architecture tidak dapat diterapkan;

0\. organizational structure tidak dapat menjalankan mandate.

⸻

21\. Success Status Model

B10 menggunakan status:

SUCCESSFUL

CONDITIONALLY SUCCESSFUL

NOT YET SUCCESSFUL

INSUFFICIENT EVIDENCE

BLOCKED

SUCCESSFUL

Seluruh critical conditions terpenuhi dan evidence memadai.

CONDITIONALLY SUCCESSFUL

Mayoritas conditions terpenuhi tetapi terdapat bounded non-critical gaps dengan remediation yang telah disetujui.

NOT YET SUCCESSFUL

Material success conditions belum terpenuhi.

INSUFFICIENT EVIDENCE

Evidence tidak cukup untuk membuat trustworthy conclusion.

BLOCKED

Success assessment tidak dapat diselesaikan karena material authority, dependency, governance, atau organizational condition belum resolved.

⸻

22\. Evidence Before Success Claim

Success claim harus mengikuti:

Success Criterion

↓

Evidence

↓

Assessment

↓

Conclusion

Tidak boleh:

Desired Outcome

↓

Success Claim

Evidence harus relevan terhadap criterion yang dinilai.

⸻

Potential evidence:

• Organizational Responsibility Map;

• Organization Architecture;

Nama atau schema evidence yang belum established source tetap diklasifikasikan sebagai evidence candidates, bukan canonical repository requirements.

↓

↓

25\. Confidence Model

Success assessment dapat menggunakan:

HIGH CONFIDENCE

MEDIUM CONFIDENCE

LOW CONFIDENCE

INSUFFICIENT EVIDENCE

Contoh:

SUCCESSFUL

-

LOW CONFIDENCE

tidak boleh diperlakukan sebagai strong final conclusion tanpa additional evidence.

⸻

26\. Organizational Gap Model

Organizational gap harus dibedakan menjadi:

Ownership Gap

Responsibility tidak memiliki owner yang jelas.

Capability Gap

Required capability belum tersedia secara memadai.

Workforce / Role Gap

Role Group atau Team belum mampu memenuhi responsibility.

Governance Gap

Authority, accountability, review, atau escalation belum memadai.

Coordination Gap

Interface atau coordination mechanism tidak efektif.

Boundary Gap

Domain ownership atau organizational boundary ambigu.

Maturity Gap

Organisasi belum mencapai maturity yang diperlukan.

⸻

27\. Gap Classification

Canonical flow:

Observed Condition

↓

Evidence

↓

Gap Identification

↓

Gap Classification

↓

Materiality

↓

Authorized Response

Gap tidak boleh langsung diterjemahkan menjadi structural redesign.

⸻

28\. Success vs Activity

B10 tidak menganggap:

• meeting selesai;

• dokumen dibuat;

• Team terbentuk;

• role ditetapkan;

• review dilakukan;

sebagai bukti success dengan sendirinya.

Activity adalah evidence candidate.

Success membutuhkan outcome.

⸻

29\. Success vs Structure

Struktur organisasi yang lengkap tidak otomatis berarti organisasi berhasil.

Structure

≠

Effectiveness

Struktur harus menghasilkan kemampuan menjalankan mandate.

⸻

30\. Success vs Compliance

Compliance terhadap governance requirement bukan keseluruhan organizational success.

Compliance

≠

Organizational Success

Compliance adalah salah satu input terhadap governance integrity.

⸻

31\. Success vs Capability Existence

Capability yang tercatat dalam organizational model tidak otomatis membuktikan capability effectiveness.

Capability Exists

≠

Capability Effective

⸻

32\. Success vs Coordination Activity

Jumlah coordination event tidak menentukan coordination effectiveness.

Assessment harus melihat:

• dependency resolution;

• information flow;

• decision clarity;

• issue resolution;

• ownership preservation;

• outcome.

⸻

33\. Success vs Maturity

High maturity tidak otomatis berarti semua organizational objectives tercapai.

Maturity

≠

Effectiveness

Maturity merupakan salah satu success dimension.

⸻

34\. Success vs Individual Performance

PD-04 organizational success tidak boleh bergantung pada penilaian satu individu.

Organizational architecture harus tetap valid apabila workforce implementation berubah.

⸻

35\. Sustainability Principle

Success hanya sustainable apabila:

• ownership tetap jelas;

• capability dapat dipertahankan;

• organizational knowledge tidak bergantung pada single individual;

• governance tetap berjalan;

• organizational interfaces tetap valid;

• material dependency tetap manageable.

⸻

36\. Regression Principle

Historical success tidak memberikan permanent immunity.

Success harus dapat direassess apabila terjadi:

• material organizational change;

• major capability change;

• ownership change;

• authority change;

• major cross-platform dependency change;

• significant governance change;

• material organizational failure;

• maturity regression.

⸻

37\. Reassessment Triggers

Reassessment dapat dipicu oleh:

Material Structural Change

OR

Material Ownership Change

OR

Material Capability Change

OR

Material Governance Change

OR

Material Cross-Platform Change

OR

Material Organizational Failure

↓

Success Reassessment

45\. Cross-Platform Success Boundary

PD-04 organizational success tidak boleh dinilai hanya dari perspektif internal PD-04 apabila material dependency lintas Platform memengaruhi mandate.

Namun cross-platform assessment tetap harus menjaga ownership.

Model:

PD-04 Internal Success

-

Cross-Platform Dependency Health

↓

PD-04 Organizational Success

PD-04 tidak menilai success Platform lain sebagai owner.

PD-04 hanya menilai dependency yang material terhadap domain-nya.

48\. Relationship to PD-05

PD-05 dapat menjadi consumer atau dependency untuk Knowledge services.

Success PD-04 harus mempertimbangkan apakah dependency tersebut menghambat Knowledge & Intelligence mandate.

PD-04 tidak mengambil Runtime ownership.

⸻

49\. Relationship to PD-06

PD-06 dapat mengonsumsi Knowledge, Context, atau Intelligence Assets.

Success PD-04 harus mempertimbangkan apakah organizational model mampu mendukung legitimate consumers.

PD-04 tidak mengambil AI Engineering ownership.

⸻

50\. Relationship to PD-07

Infrastructure dependency dapat memengaruhi organizational effectiveness.

Namun infrastructure execution tetap berada pada applicable domain owner.

⸻

51\. Relationship to PD-08

Security requirements dapat memengaruhi Knowledge organizational processes.

Security ownership tetap berada pada PD-08.

⸻

52\. Relationship to PD-09

Quality and evaluation findings dapat menjadi input bagi PD-04 organizational improvement.

PD-09 tetap menjadi applicable Quality owner.

⸻

53\. Relationship to PD-10

Developer Experience dapat menjadi consumer atau interface terhadap Knowledge services.

PD-10 tetap menjadi Developer Experience owner.

⸻

54\. Organizational Success Anti-Patterns

AP-01 — Structure Equals Success

Menganggap organisasi berhasil hanya karena struktur selesai.

⸻

AP-02 — Activity Equals Success

Menganggap aktivitas selesai sebagai proof of outcome.

⸻

AP-03 — Governance Equals Success

Menganggap compliance sebagai keseluruhan organizational success.

⸻

AP-04 — Capability Exists Equals Capability Works

Menganggap capability yang terdaftar otomatis efektif.

⸻

AP-05 — Coordination Count

Menggunakan jumlah meeting atau coordination event sebagai proxy effectiveness.

⸻

AP-06 — Ownership Drift

Mengubah dependency menjadi ownership.

⸻

AP-07 — Authority Inflation

Menggunakan success assessment untuk memperoleh authority baru.

⸻

AP-08 — Maturity Inflation

Menyatakan maturity tinggi tanpa evidence.

⸻

AP-09 — Evidence Suppression

Mengabaikan evidence yang bertentangan dengan success claim.

⸻

AP-10 — Permanent Success

Menganggap historical success tidak perlu reassessment.

⸻

63\. Canonical Freeze Statement

B10 — Knowledge & Intelligence Organizational Success establishes the terminal acceptance model for Part B — Organization Architecture of PD-04.

PD-04 organizational success requires more than structural completeness. It requires organizational effectiveness, ownership and accountability integrity, capability coverage, coordination effectiveness, governance integrity, cross-platform alignment, and organizational maturity.

PD-04 retains ownership of the Knowledge & Intelligence domain. Cross-platform dependency, governance relationship, architectural interface, or collaboration does not transfer that ownership to another Platform.

Organizational success must be supported by evidence and must remain subject to reassessment after material organizational, capability, governance, ownership, or cross-platform change.

Success is an accepted state, not permanent immunity from reassessment.

The exact B10 section is not established as literal source in the available baseline. This section is therefore explicitly classified as Bounded Canonical Reconstruction.

B10 is FROZEN WITH SOURCE QUALIFICATION.

⸻
